// =============================================================================
// worldKit.js â€” Grand Theft Aura Â· WORLD COMPILER
//
// The only module that turns world DATA into world GEOMETRY.
//
//   worldData.js      layout rows (what goes where)
//   worldObjects/     prefab definitions (what a thing is)
//   worldKit.js       <-- THIS FILE
//   worldRuntime.js   boot order + public API
//
// DESIGN RULES
//   â€¢ Colliders are ALWAYS explicit data. Never inferred from geometry.
//   â€¢ One shared unit geometry per primitive; everything else is scale.
//   â€¢ Static geometry batches into ONE InstancedMesh per
//     (geometry, material, shadow-policy, tier, chunk).
//   â€¢ Per-placement RNG is seeded from hash(id), never the global rnd() stream.
//   â€¢ Solids snap to 90Â° yaw. Decor may rotate freely.
//   â€¢ Bad rows are logged and skipped. A malformed row never kills the build.
//
// PREFAB KINDS
//   fixed shape        -> data prefab, batched into InstancedMeshes
//   needs the row      -> data prefab with build(ctx) returning a spec
//   owns runtime state -> registerBuilder (lampPosts, iceZones, vehiclesâ€¦)
// =============================================================================
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { colorToHex } from './levelData.js';
import {
  addDriveableRamp, addWorldBox, collidesHorizontally, driveableRamps, hooks,
  manholes, terrainH, worldBoxes
} from './state.js';
import { makeMat, worldRoot } from './engine.js';
import { loadTexture } from './assets.js';

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 1 â€” BUILD SESSION STATE
// =============================================================================
const KIT = {
  building: false,
  chunkSize: 128,
  spawn: [-11.5, 18.5],
  spawnKeepout: 26,
  maxDepth: 8,
  warnLimit: 40
};

let warnings = [];
const seenIds = new Set();
const placements = new Map();
const anchorsById = new Map();
const streamCells = [];
const reservations = [];
const rngCache = new Map();

function warn(msg, ...rest) {
  if (warnings.length < KIT.warnLimit) warnings.push(msg);
  console.warn('[worldKit]', msg, ...rest);
}

/** Reset every per-build accumulator. Call AFTER resetWorldState(). */
export function beginBuild(opts = {}) {
  if (opts.chunkSize) KIT.chunkSize = opts.chunkSize;
  if (opts.spawn) KIT.spawn = opts.spawn;
  if (Number.isFinite(opts.spawnKeepout)) KIT.spawnKeepout = opts.spawnKeepout;
  KIT.building = true;
  warnings = [];
  seenIds.clear();
  placements.clear();
  anchorsById.clear();
  streamCells.length = 0;
  reservations.length = 0;
  rngCache.clear();
  batches.clear();
  chunkGroups.clear();
  index.clear();
  indexedBoxes.clear();
  yawWarned.clear();
  return KIT;
}

export function kitStats() {
  let instances = 0;
  for (const b of batches.values()) instances += b.matrices.length;
  return {
    prefabs: PREFABS.size,
    builders: BUILDERS.size,
    placements: placements.size,
    batches: batches.size,
    instances,
    indexedSolids: indexedBoxes.size,
    indexCells: index.size,
    streamCells: streamCells.length,
    reservations: reservations.length,
    warnings: warnings.slice()
  };
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 2 â€” SHARED GEOMETRY
// =============================================================================
export const UNIT_BOX = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, 1, 1);
UNIT_BOX.userData.shared = true;

const geoCache = new Map();
const ownedGeos = [];

export function defineGeo(key, factory) {
  if (geoCache.has(key)) return geoCache.get(key);
  const g = factory();
  g.userData.shared = true;
  geoCache.set(key, g);
  ownedGeos.push(g);
  return g;
}

export function geo(key) {
  const g = geoCache.get(key);
  if (!g) { warn(`unknown geometry "${key}" â€” falling back to box`); return UNIT_BOX; }
  return g;
}

defineGeo('box',     () => UNIT_BOX);
defineGeo('plane',   () => new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1, 1));
defineGeo('cyl',     () => new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.5, 0.5, 1, 12));
defineGeo('cone',    () => new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.5, 1, 12));
defineGeo('sphere',  () => new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.5, 12, 8));
defineGeo('dodeca',  () => new THREE.DodecahedronGeometry(0.5, 0));
defineGeo('ico',     () => new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.5, 0));
defineGeo('octa',    () => new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.5, 0));
defineGeo('torus',   () => new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.5, 0.07, 8, 20));
defineGeo('ring',    () => new THREE.RingGeometry(0.36, 0.5, 24));
defineGeo('capsule', () => new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.25, 0.5, 6, 10));

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 3 â€” MATERIALS
// =============================================================================
const matCache = new Map();
const ownedMats = [];
const PALETTE = new Map();

export function definePalette(entries) {
  for (const [name, spec] of Object.entries(entries)) PALETTE.set(name, spec);
}

function ownMat(m) {
  m.userData.shared = true;
  ownedMats.push(m);
  return m;
}

/**
 * Resolve a prefab `m` field:
 *   'asphalt'                 -> palette entry
 *   0x313946                  -> plain cached colour
 *   { hex, texType, ...opts } -> cached makeMat
 *   THREE.Material            -> passthrough
 */
export function resolveMat(spec, fallback = 0x94a3b8) {
  if (spec && spec.isMaterial) return spec;
  if (typeof spec === 'string') {
    const ck = 'p:' + spec;
    if (matCache.has(ck)) return matCache.get(ck);
    const entry = PALETTE.get(spec);
    if (!entry) { warn(`unknown palette material "${spec}"`); return resolveMat(fallback); }
    const m = ownMat(makeMat(colorToHex(entry.hex ?? entry.color, fallback), entry.opts || entry));
    matCache.set(ck, m);
    return m;
  }
  const hex = colorToHex(spec?.hex ?? spec, fallback);
  const opts = (spec && typeof spec === 'object' && !Array.isArray(spec)) ? spec : {};
  const key = 'm:' + hex + ':' + JSON.stringify(opts);
  if (matCache.has(key)) return matCache.get(key);
  const m = ownMat(makeMat(hex, opts));
  matCache.set(key, m);
  return m;
}

// Minimal built-in palette so the kit works before worldObjects loads.

  granite:   { hex: 0x1e293b, texType: 'granite', repeatX: 3, repeatY: 1, roughness: 0.25, flatShading: false },

// --- Tiled ground materials --------------------------------------------------
const GROUND_TEXTURES = {
  grass:    loadTexture('assets/textures/grass.png',    { repeatX: 1, repeatY: 1 }),
  dirt:     loadTexture('assets/textures/dirt.png',     { repeatX: 1, repeatY: 1 }),
  rock:     loadTexture('assets/textures/rock.png',     { repeatX: 1, repeatY: 1 }),
  sand:     loadTexture('assets/textures/sand.png',     { repeatX: 1, repeatY: 1 }),
  concrete: loadTexture('assets/textures/concrete.png', { repeatX: 1, repeatY: 1 })
};
for (const tex of Object.values(GROUND_TEXTURES)) {
  tex.wrapS = tex.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.anisotropy = 8;
}
export function groundTexture(kind) { return GROUND_TEXTURES[kind] || GROUND_TEXTURES.grass; }

const groundMatCache = new Map();
export function groundMat(kind, { color = 0xffffff, repeatX = 1, repeatY = 1, roughness = 0.95, metalness = 0 } = {}) {
  const key = `${kind}|${color}|${repeatX}|${repeatY}|${roughness}|${metalness}`;
  if (groundMatCache.has(key)) return groundMatCache.get(key);
  const tex = groundTexture(kind).clone();
  tex.needsUpdate = true;
  tex.wrapS = tex.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  tex.repeat.set(repeatX, repeatY);
  const m = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color, map: tex, roughness, metalness });
  m.userData.shared = true;
  groundMatCache.set(key, m);
  return m;
}

// --- Neon signs (cached by text + colours, kit-owned) ------------------------
const signCache = new Map();
export function signMaterials(text, bgHex, textHex) {
  const key = `${text}|${bgHex}|${textHex}`;
  if (signCache.has(key)) return signCache.get(key);
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = 512; c.height = 128;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  ctx.fillStyle = bgHex;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 512, 128);
  ctx.strokeStyle = textHex;
  ctx.lineWidth = 6;
  ctx.strokeRect(6, 6, 500, 116);
  ctx.fillStyle = textHex;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  let fontSize = 42;
  for (;;) {
    ctx.font = `bold ${fontSize}px Oswald, sans-serif`;
    if (ctx.measureText(text).width <= 476 || fontSize <= 14) break;
    fontSize -= 2;
  }
  ctx.fillText(text, 256, 66);
  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.anisotropy = 4;
  const faceMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    map: tex, emissive: new THREE.Color(textHex), emissiveMap: tex,
    emissiveIntensity: 0.75, roughness: 0.25, metalness: 0.4
  });
  const sideMat = makeMat(colorToHex(bgHex, 0x0f172a), { roughness: 0.4, metalness: 0.5 });
  faceMat.userData.shared = true;
  sideMat.userData.shared = true;
  const entry = { faceMat, sideMat, tex };
  signCache.set(key, entry);
  return entry;
}

/** Six-material array for a sign box: sides, then +Z / -Z faces. */
export function signBoxMaterials(text, bgHex, textHex) {
  const { faceMat, sideMat } = signMaterials(text, bgHex, textHex);
  return [sideMat, sideMat, sideMat, sideMat, faceMat, faceMat];
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 4 â€” MESH PRIMITIVES
// =============================================================================
/**
 * makeBox(parent, w, h, d, x, y, z, material, opts)
 * opts: { cast, recv, rx, ry, rz, order, name,
 *         box: ['tag', climbable] | [x0,x1,y0,y1,z0,z1,'tag',climbable] }
 * Colliders are ALWAYS explicit; nothing is derived from the geometry.
 */
export function makeBox(parent, w, h, d, x, y, z, material, o = {}) {
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(UNIT_BOX, material);
  m.scale.set(w, h, d);
  m.position.set(x, y, z);
  if (o.rx) m.rotation.x = o.rx;
  if (o.ry) m.rotation.y = o.ry;
  if (o.rz) m.rotation.z = o.rz;
  m.castShadow = !!o.cast;
  m.receiveShadow = !!o.recv;
  if (o.order !== undefined) m.renderOrder = o.order;
  if (o.name) m.userData.studioName = o.name;
  parent.add(m);
  if (o.box) {
    const b = o.box;
    if (b.length === 2) {
      addSolidBox(x - w / 2, x + w / 2, y - h / 2, y + h / 2, z - d / 2, z + d / 2, b[0], b[1]);
    } else {
      addSolidBox(b[0], b[1], b[2], b[3], b[4], b[5], b[6], b[7]);
    }
  }
  return m;
}

/** Generic shared-geometry mesh with the same explicit-collider contract. */
export function makeMesh(parent, geoKey, material, o = {}) {
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(geo(geoKey), material);
  const p = o.p || [0, 0, 0], s = o.s || [1, 1, 1], r = o.r || [0, 0, 0];
  m.position.set(p[0], p[1], p[2]);
  m.scale.set(s[0], s[1], s[2]);
  m.rotation.set(r[0], r[1], r[2]);
  m.castShadow = !!o.cast;
  m.receiveShadow = !!o.recv;
  if (o.order !== undefined) m.renderOrder = o.order;
  if (o.name) m.userData.studioName = o.name;
  parent.add(m);
  if (o.box) {
    const b = o.box;
    if (b.length === 2) {
      addSolidBox(p[0] - s[0] / 2, p[0] + s[0] / 2,
                  p[1] - s[1] / 2, p[1] + s[1] / 2,
                  p[2] - s[2] / 2, p[2] + s[2] / 2, b[0], b[1]);
    } else {
      addSolidBox(b[0], b[1], b[2], b[3], b[4], b[5], b[6], b[7]);
    }
  }
  return m;
}

/** Compound builder. parts: [w,h,d, dx,dy,dz, material, opts?]. */
export function buildCompound(parent, bx, bz, parts, by = 0) {
  const out = [];
  for (const p of parts) {
    out.push(makeBox(parent, p[0], p[1], p[2], bx + p[3], by + p[4], bz + p[5], p[6], p[7] || {}));
  }
  return out;
}

/** Instance-matrix accumulator (shared dummy â€” sequential use only). */
const _dm = new THREE.Object3D();
export function xform(list, x, y, z, sx = 1, sy = 1, sz = 1, rx = 0, ry = 0, rz = 0) {
  _dm.position.set(x, y, z);
  _dm.rotation.set(rx, ry, rz);
  _dm.scale.set(sx, sy, sz);
  _dm.updateMatrix();
  list.push(_dm.matrix.clone());
}

export function flushInstances(parent, geometry, material, transforms, { cast = false, recv = false, name = null } = {}) {
  if (!transforms.length) return null;
  const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geometry, material, transforms.length);
  transforms.forEach((m, i) => im.setMatrixAt(i, m));
  im.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
  im.castShadow = cast;
  im.receiveShadow = recv;
  if (name) im.userData.studioName = name;
  if (im.computeBoundingSphere) im.computeBoundingSphere();
  parent.add(im);
  return im;
}

/** Squared planar distance â€” hot-loop threshold checks (no sqrt). */
export function dist2(ax, az, bx, bz) { const dx = ax - bx, dz = az - bz; return dx * dx + dz * dz; }

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 5 â€” CHUNKED INSTANCE BATCHING
// =============================================================================
const batches = new Map();
const chunkGroups = new Map();
let objIds = new WeakMap();
let nextObjId = 1;

function idOf(o) {
  let id = objIds.get(o);
  if (id === undefined) { id = nextObjId++; objIds.set(o, id); }
  return id;
}
function chunkCoord(v) { return Math.floor(v / KIT.chunkSize); }

function chunkGroup(cx, cz, tier) {
  const key = `${cx}|${cz}|${tier}`;
  let g = chunkGroups.get(key);
  if (g) return g;
  g = new THREE.Group();
  g.userData.studioName = `chunk ${cx},${cz}${tier === 'base' ? '' : ' [' + tier + ']'}`;
  g.userData.studioSelectable = false;
  g.userData.worldBuilt = true;
  chunkGroups.set(key, g);
  return g;
}

/** Queue one static instance into the world batch. */
export function inst(geometry, material, x, y, z, sx = 1, sy = 1, sz = 1, rx = 0, ry = 0, rz = 0, o = {}) {
  const cast = !!o.cast, recv = !!o.recv, tier = o.tier || 'base';
  const cx = chunkCoord(x), cz = chunkCoord(z);
  const key = `${idOf(geometry)}|${idOf(material)}|${cast ? 1 : 0}${recv ? 1 : 0}|${tier}|${cx}|${cz}`;
  let b = batches.get(key);
  if (!b) {
    b = {
      geometry, material, cast, recv, tier, cx, cz, matrices: [],
      minX: Infinity, maxX: -Infinity, minZ: Infinity, maxZ: -Infinity
    };
    batches.set(key, b);
  }
  _dm.position.set(x, y, z);
  _dm.rotation.set(rx, ry, rz);
  _dm.scale.set(sx, sy, sz);
  _dm.updateMatrix();
  b.matrices.push(_dm.matrix.clone());
  if (x < b.minX) b.minX = x;
  if (x > b.maxX) b.maxX = x;
  if (z < b.minZ) b.minZ = z;
  if (z > b.maxZ) b.maxZ = z;
}

/** Same, but from an already-composed world matrix. */
export function instMatrix(geometry, material, matrix, o = {}) {
  _dm.matrix.copy(matrix);
  _dm.matrix.decompose(_dm.position, _dm.quaternion, _dm.scale);
  const e = new THREE.Euler().setFromQuaternion(_dm.quaternion, 'YXZ');
  inst(geometry, material, _dm.position.x, _dm.position.y, _dm.position.z,
       _dm.scale.x, _dm.scale.y, _dm.scale.z, e.x, e.y, e.z, o);
}

/** Materialise every queued batch. Call once at the end of the static pass. */
export function flushWorld(parent = worldRoot) {
  let meshes = 0, instances = 0;
  const tiered = new Map();

  for (const b of batches.values()) {
    if (!b.matrices.length) continue;
    const g = chunkGroup(b.cx, b.cz, b.tier);
    if (!g.parent) parent.add(g);
    const im = flushInstances(g, b.geometry, b.material, b.matrices, { cast: b.cast, recv: b.recv });
    if (im) { meshes++; instances += b.matrices.length; }

    if (b.tier !== 'base') {
      const key = `${b.cx}|${b.cz}|${b.tier}`;
      let t = tiered.get(key);
      if (!t) {
        t = { cx: b.cx, cz: b.cz, tier: b.tier, group: g,
              minX: Infinity, maxX: -Infinity, minZ: Infinity, maxZ: -Infinity };
        tiered.set(key, t);
      }
      t.minX = Math.min(t.minX, b.minX); t.maxX = Math.max(t.maxX, b.maxX);
      t.minZ = Math.min(t.minZ, b.minZ); t.maxZ = Math.max(t.maxZ, b.maxZ);
    }
  }

  // ONE cell per (chunk, tier) â€” never one per material, or they fight over
  // the same group's visibility.
  for (const t of tiered.values()) {
    registerStreamCell({
      id: `chunk:${t.cx},${t.cz}:${t.tier}`,
      x: (t.minX + t.maxX) / 2, z: (t.minZ + t.maxZ) / 2, y: 0,
      r: Math.max(48, Math.hypot(t.maxX - t.minX, t.maxZ - t.minZ) / 2 + 32),
      mode: 'lod', group: t.group, boxes: [], tier: t.tier
    });
  }

  batches.clear();
  return { meshes, instances };
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 6 â€” COLLIDER SPATIAL HASH
// =============================================================================
const CELL = 24;
const index = new Map();
const indexedBoxes = new Set();
const boxCells = new WeakMap();

function cellKey(cx, cz) { return (cx + 32768) * 65536 + (cz + 32768); }

/** Register a solid. Same argument order and return as state.addWorldBox. */
export function addSolidBox(x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1, tag, climbable) {
  const box = addWorldBox(x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1, tag, climbable);
  indexBox(box);
  return box;
}

export function indexBox(box) {
  if (!box || indexedBoxes.has(box)) return box;
  const cx0 = Math.floor(box.minX / CELL), cx1 = Math.floor(box.maxX / CELL);
  const cz0 = Math.floor(box.minZ / CELL), cz1 = Math.floor(box.maxZ / CELL);
  const keys = [];
  for (let cx = cx0; cx <= cx1; cx++) {
    for (let cz = cz0; cz <= cz1; cz++) {
      const k = cellKey(cx, cz);
      let arr = index.get(k);
      if (!arr) { arr = []; index.set(k, arr); }
      arr.push(box);
      keys.push(k);
    }
  }
  boxCells.set(box, keys);
  indexedBoxes.add(box);
  return box;
}

/** Index anything already in worldBoxes (legacy builders, entities). */
export function reindexAll() {
  index.clear();
  indexedBoxes.clear();
  for (const b of worldBoxes) indexBox(b);
  return indexedBoxes.size;
}

export function unindexBox(box) {
  const keys = boxCells.get(box);
  if (keys) {
    for (const k of keys) {
      const arr = index.get(k);
      if (!arr) continue;
      const i = arr.indexOf(box);
      if (i !== -1) arr.splice(i, 1);
      if (!arr.length) index.delete(k);
    }
    boxCells.delete(box);
  }
  indexedBoxes.delete(box);
}

/** Remove a solid from worldBoxes AND the index. Always use this, never splice. */
export function removeSolidBox(box) {
  if (!box) return;
  const i = worldBoxes.indexOf(box);
  if (i !== -1) worldBoxes.splice(i, 1);
  unindexBox(box);
}

export function removeSolidBoxes(boxes) {
  if (!boxes) return;
  for (const b of boxes) removeSolidBox(b);
  boxes.length = 0;
}

const _qout = [];
/**
 * Candidate solids overlapping the XZ box around (x,z) with radius r.
 * May contain duplicates for multi-cell boxes â€” harmless for overlap tests
 * and max-height queries, and cheaper than de-duplicating.
 *
 * RETURNS A REUSED SCRATCH ARRAY. Never hold it across another query.
 * THE CALLER STILL APPLIES ITS OWN EXACT TEST.
 */
export function queryBoxes(x, z, r) {
  _qout.length = 0;
  const cx0 = Math.floor((x - r) / CELL), cx1 = Math.floor((x + r) / CELL);
  const cz0 = Math.floor((z - r) / CELL), cz1 = Math.floor((z + r) / CELL);
  for (let cx = cx0; cx <= cx1; cx++) {
    for (let cz = cz0; cz <= cz1; cz++) {
      const arr = index.get(cellKey(cx, cz));
      if (arr) for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) _qout.push(arr[i]);
    }
  }
  return _qout;
}

/** Reference overlap test â€” mirrors state.js semantics. */
export function collidesAt(x, z, y, h, r) {
  const y1 = y + h;
  for (const b of queryBoxes(x, z, r)) {
    if (b.minX < x + r && b.maxX > x - r &&
        b.minZ < z + r && b.maxZ > z - r &&
        b.minY < y1 && b.maxY > y) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/** Reference support query â€” highest top at or below maxY under (x,z). */
export function supportFloorAt(x, z, maxY, r) {
  let best = 0;
  for (const b of queryBoxes(x, z, r)) {
    if (b.maxY > maxY) continue;
    if (b.minX < x + r && b.maxX > x - r && b.minZ < z + r && b.maxZ > z - r) {
      if (b.maxY > best) best = b.maxY;
    }
  }
  return best;
}

/** Publish the candidate query so state.js opts in without a static import. */
export function installColliderIndex() {
  hooks.queryWorldBoxes = queryBoxes;
  reindexAll();
  return indexedBoxes.size;
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 7 â€” RESERVED FOOTPRINTS & PLACEMENT VALIDATION
// =============================================================================
export function reserveFootprint(minX, maxX, minZ, maxZ, tag = 'reserved') {
  reservations.push({ minX, maxX, minZ, maxZ, tag });
}
export function clearReservations() { reservations.length = 0; }

/** True when a wÃ—d footprint at (cx,cz) is clear of roads, props and the spawn. */
export function footprintClear(cx, cz, w, d, pad = 2.5) {
  const x0 = cx - w / 2 - pad, x1 = cx + w / 2 + pad;
  const z0 = cz - d / 2 - pad, z1 = cz + d / 2 + pad;
  for (let i = 0; i < reservations.length; i++) {
    const r = reservations[i];
    if (x0 < r.maxX && x1 > r.minX && z0 < r.maxZ && z1 > r.minZ) return false;
  }
  const stepX = Math.max(2, w / Math.max(1, Math.ceil(w / 4)));
  const stepZ = Math.max(2, d / Math.max(1, Math.ceil(d / 4)));
  for (let a = -w / 2; a <= w / 2 + 0.01; a += stepX) {
    for (let b = -d / 2; b <= d / 2 + 0.01; b += stepZ) {
      if (collidesHorizontally(cx + a, cz + b, 0.2, 1.8, 1.2)) return false;
    }
  }
  const mr = Math.max(w, d);
  for (const m of manholes) if (dist2(cx, cz, m[0], m[1]) < mr * mr) return false;
  if (dist2(cx, cz, KIT.spawn[0], KIT.spawn[1]) < KIT.spawnKeepout * KIT.spawnKeepout) return false;
  return true;
}

const yawWarned = new Set();
function checkYaw(id, ry, solids) {
  if (!ry || !solids || !solids.length) return true;
  const q = Math.abs(ry / (Math.PI / 2));
  const snapped = Math.abs(q - Math.round(q)) < 1e-4;
  if (!snapped && !yawWarned.has(id)) {
    yawWarned.add(id);
    warn(`"${id}" has solids at a non-90Â° yaw â€” collider AABB will be inflated. Snap solids, rotate decor only.`);
  }
  return snapped;
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 8 â€” DETERMINISTIC PER-PLACEMENT RNG
// =============================================================================
function hashStr(s) {
  let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
  const str = String(s);
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    h ^= str.charCodeAt(i);
    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619) >>> 0;
  }
  return h >>> 0;
}
function mulberry32(a) {
  return function () {
    a |= 0; a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a);
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}
/** Stable RNG for a placement id. Adding content elsewhere never perturbs it. */
export function rngFor(id) {
  if (rngCache.has(id)) return rngCache.get(id);
  const r = mulberry32(hashStr(id));
  rngCache.set(id, r);
  return r;
}
export function pickFrom(rng, arr) { return arr[Math.floor(rng() * arr.length) % arr.length]; }
export function rangeFrom(rng, a, b) { return a + rng() * (b - a); }

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 9 â€” TRANSFORMS
// =============================================================================
/** Placement transform: uniform scale + yaw only. Solids stay axis-aligned. */
export function xfFromRow(row) {
  const at = row.at || [0, 0, 0];
  const x = Number(at[0]) || 0;
  const z = Number(at[1]) || 0;
  const y = Number.isFinite(at[2]) ? at[2]
    : Number.isFinite(row.y) ? row.y
    : Number.isFinite(row.baseY) ? row.baseY : 0;
  return { x, y, z, ry: Number(row.ry) || 0, s: Number(row.s ?? row.scale) || 1 };
}

export function composeXf(parent, local) {
  const c = Math.cos(parent.ry), s = Math.sin(parent.ry);
  const lx = (local.x || 0) * parent.s, lz = (local.z || 0) * parent.s;
  return {
    x: parent.x + lx * c + lz * s,
    y: parent.y + (local.y || 0) * parent.s,
    z: parent.z - lx * s + lz * c,
    ry: parent.ry + (local.ry || 0),
    s: parent.s * (local.s ?? 1)
  };
}

/** Local point -> world point under a placement transform. */
export function xfPoint(xf, p) {
  const c = Math.cos(xf.ry), s = Math.sin(xf.ry);
  const lx = (p[0] || 0) * xf.s, ly = (p[1] || 0) * xf.s, lz = (p[2] || 0) * xf.s;
  return [xf.x + lx * c + lz * s, xf.y + ly, xf.z - lx * s + lz * c];
}

/** World point -> local point (inverse of xfPoint). */
export function xfInversePoint(xf, p) {
  const dx = p[0] - xf.x, dy = p[1] - xf.y, dz = p[2] - xf.z;
  const c = Math.cos(xf.ry), s = Math.sin(xf.ry);
  return [(dx * c - dz * s) / xf.s, dy / xf.s, (dx * s + dz * c) / xf.s];
}

/** Local AABB -> world AABB (exact at 90Â° yaw, inflated otherwise). */
function xfBox(xf, b) {
  const c = Math.cos(xf.ry), s = Math.sin(xf.ry);
  const ex = (Math.abs(b[1] - b[0]) / 2) * xf.s;
  const ez = (Math.abs(b[5] - b[4]) / 2) * xf.s;
  const mx = (b[0] + b[1]) / 2, mz = (b[4] + b[5]) / 2;
  const w = xfPoint(xf, [mx, 0, mz]);
  const hx = ex * Math.abs(c) + ez * Math.abs(s);
  const hz = ex * Math.abs(s) + ez * Math.abs(c);
  const y0 = xf.y + Math.min(b[2], b[3]) * xf.s;
  const y1 = xf.y + Math.max(b[2], b[3]) * xf.s;
  return [w[0] - hx, w[0] + hx, y0, y1, w[2] - hz, w[2] + hz];
}

/** Normalise a solid spec ({p,s} or {b}) into a local AABB. */
function solidLocalAABB(sp) {
  if (sp.b) return sp.b;
  const p = sp.p || [0, 0, 0], s = sp.s || [1, 1, 1];
  return [p[0] - s[0] / 2, p[0] + s[0] / 2,
          p[1] - s[1] / 2, p[1] + s[1] / 2,
          p[2] - s[2] / 2, p[2] + s[2] / 2];
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 10 â€” PREFAB REGISTRY
// =============================================================================
const PREFABS = new Map();
const BUILDERS = new Map();

/**
 * def = {
 *   kind: 'prop'|'building'|'interior'|'surface',
 *   footprint: [w,d] | (row) => [w,d],
 *   validate: bool, selectable: bool,
 *   parts:    [{ g, m, p:[x,y,z], s:[w,h,d], r:[rx,ry,rz], cast, recv, tier, name }],
 *   solids:   [{ b:[x0,x1,y0,y1,z0,z1] | p,s, tag, climb }],
 *   repeat:   [{ ref, from, step, count, ry, ryStep, s, jitter }],
 *   instances:[{ ref, at, ry, s }],
 *   anchors:  { name:[x,y,z] },
 *   stream:   { r, band },
 *   build:    (ctx) => spec     // PARAMETRIC: ctx carries the whole row + w/d/rng/xf
 * }
 */
export function definePrefab(id, def) {
  if (PREFABS.has(id)) warn(`prefab "${id}" redefined`);
  PREFABS.set(id, { id, ...def });
  return PREFABS.get(id);
}
export function definePrefabs(map) {
  for (const [id, def] of Object.entries(map)) definePrefab(id, def);
}
export function getPrefab(id) { return PREFABS.get(id) || null; }
export function hasPrefab(id) { return PREFABS.has(id) || BUILDERS.has(id); }

/**
 * Register a builder as a prefab. fn(row, ctx) where ctx carries the kit.
 * Return a THREE.Object3D, or { object, boxes, anchors, ...extra }.
 * Extra fields are preserved on record.data for worldSystems to consume.
 */
export function registerBuilder(id, fn, meta = {}) {
  BUILDERS.set(id, { id, fn, ...meta });
}
export function getBuilder(id) { return BUILDERS.get(id) || null; }

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 11 â€” THE COMPILER
// =============================================================================
function expandRepeat(rep, rng) {
  const out = [];
  const n = Math.max(0, Math.floor(rep.count || 0));
  const from = rep.from || [0, 0, 0];
  const step = rep.step || [0, 0, 0];
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const j = rep.jitter || 0;
    out.push({
      ref: rep.ref,
      at: [
        from[0] + step[0] * i + (j ? (rng() - 0.5) * j : 0),
        from[1] + step[1] * i,
        from[2] + step[2] * i + (j ? (rng() - 0.5) * j : 0)
      ],
      ry: (rep.ry || 0) + (rep.ryStep || 0) * i,
      s: rep.s ?? 1
    });
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * Flatten a prefab into world-space parts / solids / anchors.
 * `row` is the layout row (or a {w,d} shim from the streamer). Parametric
 * prefabs receive it through build(ctx); fixed prefabs ignore it.
 */
export function compilePrefab(id, xf, out, rng, depth = 0, selectable = false, row = null) {
  if (depth > KIT.maxDepth) { warn(`prefab recursion cap hit at "${id}"`); return out; }
  const def = PREFABS.get(id);
  if (!def) { warn(`unknown prefab "${id}"`); return out; }

  // Parametric prefabs describe themselves per row; fixed ones ARE the spec.
  let spec = def;
  if (typeof def.build === 'function') {
    spec = def.build({
      ...(row || {}), id, xf, rng, s: xf.s,
      w: row?.w ?? def.w ?? 16,
      d: row?.d ?? def.d ?? 16,
      geo, resolveMat, pickFrom, rangeFrom
    }) || def;
  }

  // --- parts ---------------------------------------------------------------
  for (const part of spec.parts || []) {
    const w = xfPoint(xf, part.p || [0, 0, 0]);
    const s = part.s || [1, 1, 1];
    const r = part.r || [0, 0, 0];
    out.parts.push({
      geometry: geo(part.g || 'box'),
      material: resolveMat(part.m),
      x: w[0], y: w[1], z: w[2],
      sx: s[0] * xf.s, sy: s[1] * xf.s, sz: s[2] * xf.s,
      rx: r[0], ry: xf.ry + r[1], rz: r[2],
      cast: !!part.cast, recv: !!part.recv,
      tier: row?.tier || part.tier || 'base',
      name: part.name || null
    });
  }

  // --- solids (row.solid === false opts the whole placement out) -----------
  if (row?.solid !== false) {
    for (const sp of spec.solids || []) {
      out.solids.push({
        b: xfBox(xf, solidLocalAABB(sp)),
        tag: sp.tag || 'prefab_solid',
        climb: sp.climb !== false
      });
    }
  }

  // --- anchors -------------------------------------------------------------
  for (const [name, p] of Object.entries(spec.anchors || {})) {
    out.anchors[name] = xfPoint(xf, p);
  }

  // --- nested instances (children are fixed kits â€” never forward the row) --
  const children = [...(spec.instances || [])];
  for (const rep of spec.repeat || []) children.push(...expandRepeat(rep, rng));
  for (const child of children) {
    if (!child.ref) continue;
    const a = child.at || [0, 0, 0];
    const childXf = composeXf(xf, { x: a[0], y: a[1], z: a[2], ry: child.ry || 0, s: child.s ?? 1 });
    if (BUILDERS.get(child.ref)) { out.nested.push({ id: child.ref, xf: childXf }); continue; }
    compilePrefab(child.ref, childXf, out, rng, depth + 1, selectable, null);
  }
  return out;
}

function newOut() { return { parts: [], solids: [], anchors: {}, nested: [] }; }

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 12 â€” PLACEMENT PIPELINE
// =============================================================================
export function registerStreamCell(cell) {
  streamCells.push(cell);
  if (hooks.worldStreamRegister) hooks.worldStreamRegister(cell);
  return cell;
}
export function getStreamCells() { return streamCells; }

function shouldValidate(row, def) {
  if (row.validate === false) return false;
  if (row.validate === true) return true;
  if (def?.validate !== undefined) return def.validate;
  return def?.kind === 'building';
}

function resolveFootprint(row, def, builder) {
  if (row.footprint) return row.footprint;
  const fp = def?.footprint ?? builder?.footprint;
  return typeof fp === 'function' ? fp(row) : fp;
}

/** Full builder context. `parent` is the group/world root the row places into. */
function builderCtx(row, id, xf, rng, parent) {
  return {
    rng, xf, row, id, parent, THREE,
    place, compile: compilePrefab,
    makeBox, makeMesh, buildCompound, inst, instMatrix, xform, flushInstances,
    addSolidBox, removeSolidBox, removeSolidBoxes, addRamp, removeRamp,
    reserveFootprint, footprintClear,
    resolveMat, groundMat, geo, signBoxMaterials, signMaterials,
    dist2, pickFrom, rangeFrom
  };
}

/**
 * Place one layout row. Returns a record, or null if the row was rejected.
 * Never throws â€” a malformed row is logged and skipped.
 */
export function place(row, parent = worldRoot) {
  try {
    if (!row || !row.prefab) { warn('row without prefab', row); return null; }
    const id = row.id || `${row.prefab}@${(row.at || []).join(',')}`;
    if (seenIds.has(id)) { warn(`duplicate placement id "${id}" â€” skipped`); return null; }

    const def = PREFABS.get(row.prefab);
    const builder = BUILDERS.get(row.prefab);
    if (!def && !builder) { warn(`unknown prefab "${row.prefab}" (id "${id}")`); return null; }

    const xf = xfFromRow(row);
    if (!Number.isFinite(xf.x) || !Number.isFinite(xf.z)) { warn(`bad coords on "${id}"`); return null; }

    // --- validation --------------------------------------------------------
    const fp = resolveFootprint(row, def, builder);
    if (fp && shouldValidate(row, def || builder)) {
      const c = Math.abs(Math.cos(xf.ry)), s = Math.abs(Math.sin(xf.ry));
      const w = (fp[0] * c + fp[1] * s) * xf.s;
      const d = (fp[0] * s + fp[1] * c) * xf.s;
      if (!footprintClear(xf.x, xf.z, w, d, row.pad ?? 2.5)) {
        warn(`lot rejected (blocked): ${id} @ ${xf.x},${xf.z}`);
        return null;
      }
    }
    checkYaw(id, xf.ry, def?.solids);

    seenIds.add(id);
    const rng = rngFor(id);
    const rec = { id, prefab: row.prefab, xf, row, object: null, boxes: [], anchors: {}, instances: 0, data: null };

    // --- builder path ------------------------------------------------------
    if (builder) {
      const result = builder.fn(row, builderCtx(row, id, xf, rng, parent));
      if (result && result.isObject3D) {
        rec.object = result;
      } else if (result) {
        rec.object = result.object || null;
        rec.boxes = result.boxes || [];
        rec.anchors = result.anchors || {};
        rec.data = result;   // runtime handles for worldSystems/*
      }
      if (rec.object) {
        rec.object.userData.studioKey = `static:${id}`;
        rec.object.userData.studioName = rec.object.userData.studioName || row.name || id;
      }
      placements.set(id, rec);
      if (Object.keys(rec.anchors).length) anchorsById.set(id, rec.anchors);
      return rec;
    }

    // --- data prefab path --------------------------------------------------
    const selectable = row.selectable === true || def.selectable === true || def.kind === 'interior';
    const out = compilePrefab(row.prefab, xf, newOut(), rng, 0, selectable, row);

    if (selectable) {
      // Real meshes so Studio can grab this placement individually.
      const g = new THREE.Group();
      g.position.set(xf.x, xf.y, xf.z);
      g.rotation.y = xf.ry;
      g.userData.studioName = row.name || id;
      g.userData.studioKey = `static:${id}`;
      for (const p of out.parts) {
        const lp = xfInversePoint(xf, [p.x, p.y, p.z]);
        const m = new THREE.Mesh(p.geometry, p.material);
        m.position.set(lp[0], lp[1], lp[2]);
        m.scale.set(p.sx / xf.s, p.sy / xf.s, p.sz / xf.s);
        m.rotation.set(p.rx, p.ry - xf.ry, p.rz);
        m.castShadow = p.cast;
        m.receiveShadow = p.recv;
        if (p.name) m.userData.studioName = p.name;
        g.add(m);
      }
      parent.add(g);
      rec.object = g;
    } else {
      for (const p of out.parts) {
        inst(p.geometry, p.material, p.x, p.y, p.z, p.sx, p.sy, p.sz, p.rx, p.ry, p.rz,
             { cast: p.cast, recv: p.recv, tier: p.tier });
        rec.instances++;
      }
    }

    for (const sp of out.solids) {
      rec.boxes.push(addSolidBox(sp.b[0], sp.b[1], sp.b[2], sp.b[3], sp.b[4], sp.b[5], sp.tag, sp.climb));
    }
    rec.anchors = out.anchors;
    if (Object.keys(out.anchors).length) anchorsById.set(id, out.anchors);

    // Nested builders discovered inside the data prefab.
    for (const n of out.nested) {
      const nb = BUILDERS.get(n.id);
      if (!nb) { warn(`unknown nested builder "${n.id}"`); continue; }
      const nrow = { ...row, id: `${id}/${n.id}`, at: [n.xf.x, n.xf.z], y: n.xf.y, ry: n.xf.ry, s: n.xf.s };
      nb.fn(nrow, builderCtx(nrow, nrow.id, n.xf, rng, parent));
    }

    // --- interiors become stream cells, not always-resident geometry -------
    if (def.stream || def.kind === 'interior') {
      // Hand collider ownership to the streamer: detach here so neither the
      // old nor the new worldStream can double-register them on first load.
      const handover = rec.boxes.slice();
      removeSolidBoxes(rec.boxes);
      if (rec.object && rec.object.parent) rec.object.parent.remove(rec.object);
      registerStreamCell({
        id, x: xf.x, y: xf.y, z: xf.z,
        r: def.stream?.r ?? 56,
        band: def.stream?.band ?? 0,
        mode: 'stream', tier: 'interior',
        group: rec.object, boxes: handover
      });
    }

    placements.set(id, rec);
    return rec;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[worldKit] placement failed:', row && row.id, err);
    return null;
  }
}

export function placeAll(rows, parent = worldRoot) {
  let built = 0;
  for (const row of rows || []) if (place(row, parent)) built++;
  return { built, total: (rows || []).length, rejected: (rows || []).length - built };
}

export function getPlacement(id) { return placements.get(id) || null; }
export function allPlacements() { return placements; }

/** World position of a named anchor on a placed prefab. */
export function anchor(id, name) {
  const a = anchorsById.get(id);
  return a ? (a[name] || null) : null;
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 13 â€” DRIVEABLE RAMPS
// =============================================================================
export function addRamp(spec) { return addDriveableRamp(spec); }
export function removeRamp(ref) {
  const i = driveableRamps.indexOf(ref);
  if (i !== -1) driveableRamps.splice(i, 1);
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 14 â€” DISPOSAL
// =============================================================================
/** Dispose a subtree, skipping kit-owned shared geometry/materials. */
export function disposeTree(root) {
  if (!root) return;
  const seenGeo = new Set(), seenMat = new Set();
  root.traverse((o) => {
    if (o.geometry && !o.geometry.userData.shared && !seenGeo.has(o.geometry)) {
      seenGeo.add(o.geometry);
      o.geometry.dispose();
    }
    const mats = Array.isArray(o.material) ? o.material : (o.material ? [o.material] : []);
    for (const m of mats) {
      if (!m || m.userData.shared || seenMat.has(m)) continue;
      seenMat.add(m);
      if (m.map && m.map.isCanvasTexture) m.map.dispose();
      if (m.emissiveMap && m.emissiveMap.isCanvasTexture && m.emissiveMap !== m.map) m.emissiveMap.dispose();
      m.dispose();
    }
  });
}

/** Release everything the kit owns. Full teardown only, not per rebuild. */
export function disposeKit() {
  for (const g of ownedGeos) if (g !== UNIT_BOX) g.dispose();
  ownedGeos.length = 0;
  geoCache.clear();
  defineGeo('box', () => UNIT_BOX);

  for (const m of ownedMats) {
    if (m.map && m.map.isCanvasTexture) m.map.dispose();
    m.dispose();
  }
  ownedMats.length = 0;
  matCache.clear();
  PALETTE.clear();

  for (const m of groundMatCache.values()) { if (m.map) m.map.dispose(); m.dispose(); }
  groundMatCache.clear();

  for (const { faceMat, sideMat, tex } of signCache.values()) {
    tex.dispose(); faceMat.dispose(); sideMat.dispose();
  }
  signCache.clear();

  batches.clear();
  chunkGroups.clear();
  index.clear();
  indexedBoxes.clear();
  objIds = new WeakMap();
  nextObjId = 1;
}

/** Per-rebuild reset that keeps cached materials (reused every build). */
export function resetKitSession() {
  batches.clear();
  chunkGroups.clear();
  index.clear();
  indexedBoxes.clear();
  placements.clear();
  anchorsById.clear();
  seenIds.clear();
  streamCells.length = 0;
  reservations.length = 0;
  rngCache.clear();
  yawWarned.clear();
  warnings = [];
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 15 â€” CATALOG GENERATION
// =============================================================================
/**
 * Regenerate the <catalog> block at the top of worldData.js so the vocabulary
 * an AI reads can never drift from what the compiler actually accepts.
 */
export function generateCatalog() {
  const lines = [];
  lines.push('// <catalog generated="true">  DO NOT EDIT â€” regenerate with `npm run catalog`');
  lines.push('// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------');

  const groups = new Map();
  for (const def of PREFABS.values()) {
    const kind = (def.kind || 'misc').toUpperCase();
    if (!groups.has(kind)) groups.set(kind, []);
    groups.get(kind).push(def);
  }
  for (const [kind, defs] of [...groups.entries()].sort()) {
    lines.push(`// ${kind}`);
    for (const def of defs.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id))) {
      const params = (def.params || []).join(', ');
      const fp = Array.isArray(def.footprint) ? ` ${def.footprint[0]}Ã—${def.footprint[1]}` : '';
      const flags = [];
      if (def.solids?.length) flags.push('solid');
      if (def.build) flags.push('parametric');
      if (def.selectable) flags.push('selectable');
      if (def.kind === 'interior') flags.push('streamed');
      if (def.anchors && Object.keys(def.anchors).length) flags.push('anchors: ' + Object.keys(def.anchors).join('|'));
      lines.push(`//   ${def.id.padEnd(20)} { ${params} }${fp}${flags.length ? ' Â· ' + flags.join(' Â· ') : ''}`);
      if (def.doc) lines.push(`//     ${def.doc}`);
    }
    lines.push('//');
  }

  if (BUILDERS.size) {
    lines.push('// BUILDER-BACKED');
    for (const b of [...BUILDERS.values()].sort((a, b2) => a.id.localeCompare(b2.id))) {
      const params = (b.params || []).join(', ');
      const fp = Array.isArray(b.footprint) ? ` ${b.footprint[0]}Ã—${b.footprint[1]}` : '';
      lines.push(`//   ${b.id.padEnd(20)} { ${params} }${fp}`);
      if (b.doc) lines.push(`//     ${b.doc}`);
    }
    lines.push('//');
  }

  lines.push('// RULES: every placement is validated against reserved footprints + colliders.');
  lines.push('//        ids must be unique and stable. ry snaps to 90Â° for solids.');
  lines.push('//        scatter/avoid bounds are [x0,z0,x1,z1]; water/patch/panel are [x0,x1,z0,z1].');
  lines.push('// </catalog>');
  return lines.join('\n');
}

// =============================================================================

// =============================================================================
// worldRuntime.js â€” Grand Theft Aura Â· BOOT, DISPOSAL & PUBLIC API
//
// This is the ONLY module the rest of the game imports. It contains no map
// data and no geometry: it consumes world.js, drives worldKit, and wires the
// systems together.
//
//   world.js            layout rows            (what goes where)
//   worldObjects/*      prefab vocabulary      (what a thing is)
//   worldTerrain.js     heightfields + water
//   worldRoads.js       carriageways
//   worldKit.js         rows -> geometry       (the compiler)
//   worldStream.js       distance/height LOD
//   worldSystems/*      behaviour
//   worldRuntime.js     <-- THIS FILE
//
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// PENDING MODULE CONTRACTS
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// worldObjects/street.js must export:
//   STREET_BUILDERS            { prop_lamppost, prop_hydrant, prop_shelter,
//                                prop_cart, prop_fountain, prop_pier,
//                                prop_lifeguard, prop_campfire, prop_tent,
//                                prop_sign_arch, prop_gantry, prop_jet,
//                                prop_service, prop_cliff, prop_dumpster,
//                                prop_ferris_wheel, prop_fire_escape, ice_zones }
//   buildKnockableLamppost(x,z), knockOverLamppostBase(lp,yaw,speed),
//   knockOverLamppost(lp,yaw,speed), updateLampposts(dt,elapsed),
//   createTree(x,z,s,y), createHydrant(x,z), createBusShelter(x,z,ry),
//   buildPlazaHotdogCart(x,z), streetFX, resetStreet(), disposeStreet()
//
// worldSystems/index.js must export:
//   systems                    { sewer, armory, estates, airport, atmosphere,
//                                edgeBarrier, towerLift }
//                              each: { install?(ctx), update?(dt,elapsed),
//                                      reset?(), build?(spec) }
//   updateWorldSystems(dt, elapsed), resetWorldSystems(),
//   buildSewer(row), bio, bioC,
//   buildEdgeBarrier(spec), updateEdgeBarrier(), revealEdgeBarrier()
//
// SYSTEM INSTALL ORDER IS LOAD-BEARING: estates installs before armory,
// because armory CHAINS aura.ahF / aura.ahHint onto whatever estates left.
// =============================================================================

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 3 â€” SYMBOLIC COORDINATES & HEIGHTS
// world.js may write 'riverX', 'ahRz+43', 'maxTerrain+0.12', 'lodgeY'.
// Everything is resolved to a NUMBER here, before worldKit sees the row.
// =============================================================================
const heightRefs = new Map();
export function defineWorldHeightRef(name, fn) { heightRefs.set(name, fn); }

/** The Frostpine loop station nearest the creek â€” the stone bridge crossing. */
function frostLoopCreekStation() {
  const loop = getPlacement('rd/frost_loop')?.data;
  if (!loop?.pts) return null;
  let best = null, bd = Infinity;
  for (const p of loop.pts) {
    if (p.z < 270 || p.z > 316) continue;
    const d = Math.abs(p.x - frostCreekX(p.z));
    if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = p; }
  }
  return best ? { ...best, dy: loop.dy, halfW: loop.halfW } : null;
}

function lodgeFounding() {
  const a = anchor('fp/lodge', 'floor');
  if (a) return a[1];
  const r = PLACEMENTS.find((p) => p.id === 'fp/lodge');
  if (!r) return terrainH(-70, 139) + 0.12;
  const w = r.w ?? 11, d = r.d ?? 9;
  return maxTerrainOver(r.at[0] - w / 2, r.at[0] + w / 2, r.at[1] - d / 2, r.at[1] + d / 2) + 0.12;
}

function resolveHeight(spec, row, x, z) {
  if (Number.isFinite(spec)) return spec;
  if (typeof spec !== 'string') return undefined;
  const s = spec.trim();
  if (s === 'terrain') return terrainH(x, z);
  if (s === 'lodgeY') return lodgeFounding();

  let m = /^maxTerrain([+-][\d.]+)?$/.exec(s);
  if (m) {
    const w = row.w ?? 10, d = row.d ?? 10;
    return maxTerrainOver(x - w / 2, x + w / 2, z - d / 2, z + d / 2) + (m[1] ? Number(m[1]) : 0);
  }
  m = /^river([+-][\d.]+)?$/.exec(s);
  if (m) return riverSurfaceAt(row.river, z) + (m[1] ? Number(m[1]) : 0.3);

  m = /^max\(creek,loop\)([+-][\d.]+)?$/.exec(s);
  if (m) {
    const st = row._loopStation || frostLoopCreekStation();
    const creek = riverSurfaceAt('wat/frostpine_creek', z);
    const road = st ? st.y + st.dy : terrainH(x, z);
    return Math.max(creek, road) + (m[1] ? Number(m[1]) : 0.3);
  }
  const fn = heightRefs.get(s);
  if (fn) { const v = fn(); if (Number.isFinite(v)) return v; }
  console.warn('[worldRuntime] unresolved height:', s, 'on', row.id);
  return undefined;
}

/**
 * Resolve one layout row into pure numbers. Never mutates the source data â€”
 * world.js stays a pristine description that can be re-read every rebuild.
 */
function prepRow(row) {
  if (!row) return row;
  let r = row;

  // 'loopCross' is not a path function â€” substitute it before coord resolution.
  if (Array.isArray(r.at) && r.at[1] === 'loopCross') {
    const st = frostLoopCreekStation();
    r = { ...r, at: [r.at[0], st ? st.z : 292], _loopStation: st };
  }

  if (Array.isArray(r.at) && r.at.some((v) => typeof v === 'string')) {
    const { x, z } = resolveRowXZ(r);
    r = { ...r, at: [x, z, r.at[2]] };
  }

  const x = Number(r.at?.[0]) || 0, z = Number(r.at?.[1]) || 0;
  for (const key of ['y', 'baseY', 'deckY', 'startY', 'endY']) {
    if (typeof r[key] === 'string') {
      const v = resolveHeight(r[key], r, x, z);
      if (Number.isFinite(v)) r = { ...r, [key]: v };
    }
  }
  return r;
}

function placeRow(row, parent) { return place(prepRow(row), parent); }
function placeRows(rows, parent) {
  let built = 0;
  for (const row of rows || []) if (placeRow(row, parent)) built++;
  return built;
}

// =============================================================================

  sunnyvale: 'district:sunnyvale',
  losaura_downtown: 'district:boardwalk+lax+speedway',

  boardwalk: 'district:boardwalk+lax+speedway',

const SCATTER_STEP = {
  'sc/frost_': 'biome:frostpine+woods+cove',
  'sc/woods_': 'biome:frostpine+woods+cove',
  'sc/cove_': 'biome:frostpine+woods+cove',
  'sc/jungle_': 'biome:jungle',
  'sc/mtn_': 'terrain:mountains',
  'sc/hills_': 'district:westside'
};
/** Rows whose order inside a step is load-bearing (keep-outs, height refs). */
const STEP_FIRST = {
  'biome:frostpine+woods+cove': [
    'rd/frost_loop', 'rd/frost_spur', 'fp/lodge', 'rd/lodge_drive', 'rd/lodge_ramp', 'br/frost'
  ],
  'terrain:mountains': ['gnd/north_range', 'wat/aura_river', 'br/aura_1', 'br/aura_2'],
  'district:boardwalk+lax+speedway': ['gnd/northland', 'rd/lax_curb', 'lax/terminal']
};

const longestPrefix = (table, id) => {
  let best = null, len = -1;
  for (const k of Object.keys(table)) {
    if (id.startsWith(k) && k.length > len) { best = table[k]; len = k.length; }
  }
  return best;
};
const rowsForStep = (table, list, step) =>
  (list || []).filter((r) => longestPrefix(table, r.id) === step);
const scatterSpecsForStep = (step) =>
  SCATTERS.filter((s) => longestPrefix(SCATTER_STEP, s.id) === step);

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 5 â€” REGISTRATION
// =============================================================================
let registered = false;
function registerEverything() {
  if (registered) return;
  registered = true;

  registerTerrainPrefabs();

  registerLegacyBuilders({
    ...BUILDING_BUILDERS,
    ...ESTATE_BUILDERS,
    ...AIRPORT_BUILDERS,
    ...TOWER_BUILDERS,
    ...ROAD_BUILDERS,
    ...STREET_BUILDERS,
    sewer_network: buildSewer,
    vehicle: buildSportsCar,
    bus: systems.bus?.build || null,
    // The tower adapter calls this to register per-floor stream cells.
    registerTowerFloors: (rec, row, meta) =>
      streamRegisterTowerFloors(rec, row, meta || towerMetrics(row), expandTowerFloors)
  });

  // Symbolic heights the road module may encounter.
  defineHeightRef('lodgeY', lodgeFounding);
  defineWorldHeightRef('lodgeY', lodgeFounding);

  initWorldStream({
    defaultOn: WORLD.stream?.lodOn ?? 54,
    defaultOff: WORLD.stream?.lodOff ?? 66,
    loadPerFrame: WORLD.stream?.loadPerFrame ?? 3,
    unloadPerFrame: WORLD.stream?.unloadPerFrame ?? 6,
    urgentRadius: WORLD.stream?.urgentRadius ?? 14,
    parent: worldRoot
  });
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 6 â€” BOOT STEPS
// =============================================================================
function runStep(step, ctx) {
  const { data, register, afterStatic } = ctx;

  // ---- ordered lead rows -------------------------------------------------
  const first = STEP_FIRST[step];
  const placedIds = new Set();
  if (first) {
    for (const id of first) {
      const row = [...TERRAIN, ...WATER, ...ROADS, ...PLACEMENTS].find((r) => r.id === id);
      if (row && placeRow(row)) placedIds.add(id);
    }
  }
  const notYet = (r) => !placedIds.has(r.id);

  switch (step) {
    case 'terrain':
      placeRows(rowsForStep(TERRAIN_STEP, TERRAIN, step).filter(notYet));
      break;

    case 'roads:city+desert':
      placeRows(rowsForStep(ROAD_STEP, ROADS, step).filter(notYet));
      mapShapes.push({ x: 280, z: 10, w: 220, d: 396, color: '#d97706', border: '#92400e', enterable: false });
      break;

    case 'biome:redrock':
      placeRows(rowsForStep(PLACEMENT_STEP, PLACEMENTS, step).filter(notYet));
      break;

    case 'biome:frostpine+woods+cove': {
      placeRows(rowsForStep(TERRAIN_STEP, TERRAIN, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(WATER_STEP, WATER, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(ROAD_STEP, ROADS, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(PLACEMENT_STEP, PLACEMENTS, step).filter(notYet));
      // Keep-outs are complete â€” the forest may now grow.
      placeRows(expandScatters(scatterSpecsForStep(step)));
      mapShapes.unshift({ x: -12, z: 158, w: 80, d: 100, color: '#166534', border: '#14532d', enterable: false });
      mapShapes.unshift({ x: 83, z: 158, w: 110, d: 100, color: '#fcd34d', border: '#f59e0b', enterable: false });
      mapShapes.push({ x: -420, z: 224, w: 110, d: 180, color: '#38bdf8', border: '#0ea5e9', enterable: false });
      break;
    }

    case 'biome:jungle':
      placeRows(rowsForStep(TERRAIN_STEP, TERRAIN, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(WATER_STEP, WATER, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(ROAD_STEP, ROADS, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(PLACEMENT_STEP, PLACEMENTS, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(expandScatters(scatterSpecsForStep(step)));
      mapShapes.unshift({ x: 450.5, z: 30, w: 165, d: 420, color: '#14532d', border: '#166534', enterable: false });
      break;

    case 'district:sunnyvale':
      placeRows(expandDistricts(DISTRICTS.filter((d) => DISTRICT_STEP[d.id] === step)));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(PLACEMENT_STEP, PLACEMENTS, step).filter(notYet));
      mapShapes.push({ x: 0, z: 0, w: 30, d: 30, color: '#22c55e', border: '#15803d', enterable: false });
      break;

    case 'entities':
      spawnLevelEntities(data, { register });
      break;

    case 'afterStatic':
      if (afterStatic) afterStatic();
      break;

    case 'terrain:mountains':
      placeRows(rowsForStep(TERRAIN_STEP, TERRAIN, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(WATER_STEP, WATER, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(ROAD_STEP, ROADS, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(expandScatters(scatterSpecsForStep(step)));
      camera.far = Math.max(camera.far, 620);
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
      break;

    case 'system:sewer':
      placeRow({ id: 'sys/sewer', prefab: 'sewer_network', ...SYSTEMS.sewer });
      systems.sewer?.install?.({ spec: SYSTEMS.sewer });
      break;

    case 'district:aura_hills+estates':
      placeRows(rowsForStep(TERRAIN_STEP, TERRAIN, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(ROAD_STEP, ROADS, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(PLACEMENT_STEP, PLACEMENTS, step).filter(notYet));
      systems.estates?.install?.({ spec: SYSTEMS.estates });
      break;

    case 'district:westside':
      placeRows(rowsForStep(ROAD_STEP, ROADS, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(PLACEMENT_STEP, PLACEMENTS, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(expandScatters(scatterSpecsForStep(step)));
      break;

    case 'placement:hideout':
      placeRows(rowsForStep(PLACEMENT_STEP, PLACEMENTS, step).filter(notYet));
      systems.hideout?.install?.({ spec: SYSTEMS.hideout });
      break;

    case 'district:boardwalk+lax+speedway':
      placeRows(rowsForStep(TERRAIN_STEP, TERRAIN, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(WATER_STEP, WATER, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(ROAD_STEP, ROADS, step).filter(notYet));
      placeRows(expandDistricts(DISTRICTS.filter((d) => DISTRICT_STEP[d.id] === step)));
      placeRows(rowsForStep(PLACEMENT_STEP, PLACEMENTS, step).filter(notYet));
      systems.airport?.install?.({ spec: SYSTEMS.airport });
      systems.speedway?.install?.({ spec: SYSTEMS.speedway });
      mapShapes.unshift({ x: 120, z: 655, w: 740, d: 550, color: '#4d7c0f', border: '#365314', enterable: false });
      mapShapes.push({ x: 120, z: 299, w: 760, d: 162, color: '#0284c7', border: '#38bdf8', enterable: false });
      camera.far = Math.max(camera.far, WORLD.cameraFar ?? 1150);
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
      break;

    case 'placement:armory': {
      const rows = rowsForStep(PLACEMENT_STEP, PLACEMENTS, step).filter(notYet);
      // The shell needs the ammo config so the crate lands at the configured spot.
      for (const r of rows) {
        placeRow(r.prefab === 'shop_armory' ? { ...r, ammo: SYSTEMS.armory.ammo } : r);
      }
      systems.armory?.install?.({ spec: SYSTEMS.armory });
      break;
    }

    case 'towers': {
      const rows = rowsForStep(PLACEMENT_STEP, PLACEMENTS, step).filter(notYet);
      const built = placeRows(rows);
      console.log(`[towers] ${built}/${rows.length} towers placed`);
      systems.towerLift?.install?.();
      break;
    }

    case 'groundIntegrity':
      buildGroundIntegrity(SYSTEMS.groundIntegrity, worldRoot);
      break;

    case 'water:seabed':
      placeRows(rowsForStep(WATER_STEP, WATER, step).filter(notYet));
      break;

    case 'recomputeBounds':
      recomputeWorldBounds();
      break;

    case 'edgeBarrier':
      buildEdgeBarrier(SYSTEMS.edgeBarrier);
      break;

    case 'registerStudio':
      registerGeneratedWorldObjects(data);
      break;

    default:
      console.warn('[worldRuntime] unknown BOOT step:', step);
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 7 â€” BUILD WORLD
// =============================================================================
/**
 * Idempotent. Safe to call again from Studio / Save & Test.
 * @param {object} data      levelData (entities + worldTransforms)
 * @param {boolean} register false = preview meshes, no gameplay registration
 * @param {Function} afterStatic  called after the entity pass, before mountains
 */
export function buildWorld(data = levelData, { register = true, afterStatic = null } = {}) {
  if (!foreignRoots) foreignRoots = new Set(worldRoot.children);

  clearBuiltWorld();
  registerEverything();

  beginBuild({
    chunkSize: WORLD.chunkSize ?? 128,
    spawn: WORLD.spawn ?? [-11.5, 18.5],
    spawnKeepout: WORLD.spawnKeepout ?? 26
  });

  camera.far = Math.max(camera.far, WORLD.cameraFar ?? 1150);
  camera.updateProjectionMatrix();

  const ctx = { data, register, afterStatic };
  for (const step of BOOT) runStep(step, ctx);

  // ---- finalise ----------------------------------------------------------
  const flushed = flushWorld(worldRoot);
  installColliderIndex();

  adoptKitCells();
  adoptBuildingLods();
  adoptAuraCulls();
  adoptSkyscraperInteriors(makeTowerLobby);

  // Refill zones are published by the shop/cart builders, not written by them.
  for (const rec of allPlacements().values()) {
    if (rec.data?.refill) sausageRefillPush(rec.data.refill);
  }

  systems.atmosphere?.install?.({ spec: SYSTEMS.atmosphere });
  recomputeWorldBounds();
  updateWorldStream();

  builtOnce = true;
  lastBootReport = {
    ...kitStats(), ...flushed, stream: streamStats(),
    bounds: { ...WORLD_BOUNDS }, entities: entityObjects.size, studio: studioObjects.size
  };
  if (lastBootReport.warnings?.length) {
    console.warn(`[worldRuntime] ${lastBootReport.warnings.length} row warning(s)`, lastBootReport.warnings);
  }
  return lastBootReport;
}

function sausageRefillPush(zone) {
  // Imported lazily to avoid a cycle: state.js owns the array.
  const { sausageRefillZones } = requireState();
  if (zone && !sausageRefillZones.includes(zone)) sausageRefillZones.push(zone);
}
let _stateMod = null;
function requireState() {
  if (!_stateMod) _stateMod = { sausageRefillZones: sausageRefillZonesRef };
  return _stateMod;
}
import { sausageRefillZones as sausageRefillZonesRef } from './state.js';

export function bootReport() { return lastBootReport; }
export function worldCatalog() { return generateCatalog(); }

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 8 â€” DISPOSAL
// =============================================================================
function pruneDetachedHighDetail() {
  for (let i = highDetailObjects.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    let o = highDetailObjects[i], attached = false;
    while (o) { if (o === scene) { attached = true; break; } o = o.parent; }
    if (!attached) highDetailObjects.splice(i, 1);
  }
}

function clearBuiltWorld() {
  disposeTowerInteriors();
  resetWorldStream();

  for (const child of [...worldRoot.children]) {
    if (foreignRoots && foreignRoots.has(child)) continue;
    worldRoot.remove(child);
    disposeTree(child);
  }

  resetTowerSystem();
  resetTerrain();
  resetRoads();
  resetEstates();
  resetAirport();
  resetStreet();
  resetWorldSystems();
  resetWaterSystem();
  lastWaterFrame = -Infinity;

  resetWorldState();          // clears worldBoxes, zones, lampPosts, auraâ€¦
  resetKitSession();          // clears the collider index + placements
  clearKeepouts();
  heightRefs.clear();

  vehicles.length = 0;
  entityObjects.clear();
  studioObjects.clear();
  studioBaseTransforms.clear();
  studioDeletedObjects.clear();

  bio.base = null; bio.snow = null; bio.last = 'city';
  if (streetFX) { streetFX.fire = null; streetFX.waterfall = null; streetFX.wheel = null; }
  pruneDetachedHighDetail();
}

/** Full teardown â€” releases GPU caches. Call on app shutdown, not per rebuild. */
export function disposeWorld() {
  clearBuiltWorld();
  disposeStreet();
  disposeRoads();
  disposeTerrain();
  disposeKit();
  registered = false;
  builtOnce = false;
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 9 â€” STUDIO REGISTRY
// Stable keys: `entity:<id>` for level entities, `static:<placement id>` for
// generated geometry. Batched chunk groups are deliberately not selectable.
// =============================================================================
function applyObjectTransform(object, t) {
  if (!t || t.deleted) return;
  object.position.fromArray(t.position);
  object.rotation.fromArray(t.rotation);
  object.scale.fromArray(t.scale);
}

function registerStudioObjectTree(root, rootPath, data = levelData) {
  const visit = (object, path) => {
    object.userData.studioPath = path;
    object.userData.studioNamed = Boolean(object.userData.studioName || object.name);
    if (!object.userData.studioName) {
      object.userData.studioName = object.name || `${object.isMesh ? 'Mesh' : object.type} ${path.split('/').pop()}`;
    }
    studioObjects.set(path, object);
    if (!studioBaseTransforms.has(path)) {
      studioBaseTransforms.set(path, {
        position: object.position.toArray(),
        rotation: object.rotation.toArray(),
        scale: object.scale.toArray()
      });
    }
    const saved = data.worldTransforms && data.worldTransforms[path];
    if (saved && !saved.deleted) applyObjectTransform(object, saved);
    object.children.forEach((child, i) => visit(child, `${path}/${i}`));
  };
  visit(root, rootPath);
}

function registerGeneratedWorldObjects(data = levelData) {
  studioObjects.clear();
  studioDeletedObjects.clear();
  const usedKeys = new Map();
  const slug = (s) => String(s || 'object').toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ').replace(/^ | $/g, '').slice(0, 48) || 'object';

  for (const root of worldRoot.children) {
    if (foreignRoots && foreignRoots.has(root)) continue;
    root.userData.worldBuilt = true;
    if (root.userData.studioSelectable === false || root.userData.persistentFX) continue;

    const entityId = root.userData.entityId;
    let key;
    if (entityId) key = `entity:${entityId}`;
    else if (root.userData.studioKey) key = root.userData.studioKey;   // set by worldKit
    else {
      const base = `static:${slug(root.userData.studioName || root.name || root.type)}`;
      const n = usedKeys.get(base) || 0;
      usedKeys.set(base, n + 1);
      key = n === 0 ? base : `${base}#${n}`;
    }
    registerStudioObjectTree(root, key, data);
  }
  if (data.worldTransforms) {
    for (const [path, saved] of Object.entries(data.worldTransforms)) {
      if (saved && saved.deleted) removeEntityObject(path);
    }
  }
}

export function applyStudioWorldTransforms(data = levelData) {
  for (const [path, entry] of [...studioDeletedObjects.entries()]) {
    const saved = data.worldTransforms && data.worldTransforms[path];
    if (!saved || !saved.deleted) {
      if (entry.parent && entry.object) entry.parent.add(entry.object);
      studioDeletedObjects.delete(path);
      if (entry.object) registerStudioObjectTree(entry.object, path, data);
    }
  }
  for (const [path, object] of [...studioObjects.entries()]) {
    const saved = data.worldTransforms && data.worldTransforms[path];
    if (saved && saved.deleted) { removeEntityObject(path); continue; }
    const base = studioBaseTransforms.get(path);
    if (base) applyObjectTransform(object, base);
    if (saved) applyObjectTransform(object, saved);
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 10 â€” ENTITY BRIDGE
// =============================================================================
function tagEntity(root, id) {
  root.userData.entityId = id;
  root.traverse((c) => { c.userData.entityId = id; });
}
function applyEntityLook(object, entity) {
  if (entity.color && !['vehicle', 'block', 'prop', 'ramp', 'building', 'road'].includes(entity.type)) {
    applyEntityColor(object, entity.color);
  }
  if (entity.texture) {
    applyEntityTexture(object, entity.texture, (entity.texRepeat || [1, 1])[0], (entity.texRepeat || [1, 1])[1]);
  }
}

function makeRampMesh(color) {
  const shape = new THREE.Shape();
  shape.moveTo(-0.5, 0); shape.lineTo(0.5, 0); shape.lineTo(0.5, 1); shape.closePath();
  const g = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(shape, { depth: 1, bevelEnabled: false });
  g.translate(0, 0, -0.5);
  g.rotateY(-Math.PI / 2);
  g.computeVertexNormals();
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(g, makeMat(colorToHex(color, 0xf59e0b), { roughness: 0.7 }));
  m.castShadow = m.receiveShadow = true;
  return m;
}
function makePrimitiveMesh(shape, color, fallback) {
  const key = shape === 'cylinder' ? 'cyl' : shape === 'sphere' ? 'sphere' : shape === 'cone' ? 'cone' : 'box';
  const m = makeMesh(new THREE.Group(), key, makeMat(colorToHex(color, fallback), { roughness: 0.8 }),
    { cast: true, recv: true });
  m.parent.remove(m);
  return m;
}
function makeBuildingMesh(entity) {
  const b = entity.building || {};
  const width = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Number(b.width) || 12, 2, 200);
  const depth = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Number(b.depth) || 10, 2, 200);
  const height = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Number(b.height) || 12, 2, 300);
  const wallMat = makeMat(colorToHex(entity.color, 0x64748b), { texType: 'concrete', roughness: 0.82 });
  const trimMat = makeMat(colorToHex(b.roofColor, 0x1e293b), { metalness: 0.35, roughness: 0.55 });
  const winHex = colorToHex(b.windowColor, 0x7dd3fc);
  const glassMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    color: winHex, emissive: winHex, emissiveIntensity: 0.12, metalness: 0.2, roughness: 0.25
  });
  const root = new THREE.Group();
  makeBox(root, width, height, depth, 0, height / 2, 0, wallMat, { cast: true, recv: true, name: 'Building body' });
  makeBox(root, width + 0.5, 0.35, depth + 0.5, 0, height + 0.17, 0, trimMat, { cast: true, name: 'Roof' });
  const floors = Math.min(20, Math.max(1, Math.floor(height / 3.2)));
  const columns = Math.min(8, Math.max(1, Math.floor(width / 2.8)));
  const winW = Math.min(1.35, width / (columns * 2.2));
  const winH = Math.min(1.45, Math.max(0.55, height / (floors * 2.1)));
  const t = [];
  for (let f = 0; f < floors; f++) {
    const y = (f + 0.55) * height / floors;
    for (let c = 0; c < columns; c++) {
      const x = (c + 0.5) * width / columns - width / 2;
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) xform(t, x, y, s * (depth / 2 + 0.035), winW, winH, 0.06);
    }
  }
  const win = flushInstances(root, UNIT_BOX, glassMat, t);
  if (win) win.userData.studioName = 'Windows';
  return root;
}
function makeRoadMesh(entity) {
  const road = entity.road || {};
  const width = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Number(road.width) || 24, 2, 500);
  const depth = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Number(road.depth) || 12, 2, 500);
  const root = new THREE.Group();
  makeBox(root, width, 0.12, depth, 0, 0.06, 0,
    makeMat(colorToHex(entity.color, 0x313946), { texType: 'asphalt', roughness: 0.88 }), { recv: true });
  if (road.markings !== false) {
    makeBox(root, 0.22, 0.025, depth * 0.78, 0, 0.13, 0, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfacc15 }));
  }
  return root;
}

function registerRoadRamp(object, entity) {
  object.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  const bounds = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(object);
  const rot = entity.rot || [0, 0, 0], scale = entity.scale || [1, 1, 1];
  if (Math.hypot(rot[0] || 0, rot[2] || 0) === 0) {
    return addSolidBox(bounds.min.x, bounds.max.x, bounds.min.y, bounds.max.y,
      bounds.min.z, bounds.max.z, 'entity_road', true);
  }
  const cfg = entity.road || {};
  const halfW = ((Number(cfg.width) || 24) * scale[0]) * 0.5;
  const halfL = ((Number(cfg.depth) || 12) * scale[2]) * 0.5;
  const pNX = new THREE.Vector3(-halfW, 0, 0).applyMatrix4(object.matrixWorld);
  const pPX = new THREE.Vector3(halfW, 0, 0).applyMatrix4(object.matrixWorld);
  const pNZ = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -halfL).applyMatrix4(object.matrixWorld);
  const pPZ = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, halfL).applyMatrix4(object.matrixWorld);
  const useX = Math.abs(pPX.y - pNX.y) >= Math.abs(pPZ.y - pNZ.y);
  const pMin = useX ? (pNX.x < pPX.x ? pNX : pPX) : (pNZ.z < pPZ.z ? pNZ : pPZ);
  const pMax = useX ? (pNX.x < pPX.x ? pPX : pNX) : (pNZ.z < pPZ.z ? pPZ : pNZ);
  return addRamp({
    minX: bounds.min.x, maxX: bounds.max.x, minZ: bounds.min.z, maxZ: bounds.max.z,
    startY: pMin.y + 0.08, endY: pMax.y + 0.08, axis: useX ? 'x' : 'z', dir: pMax.y >= pMin.y ? 1 : -1
  });
}
function registerEntityRamp(object, entity) {
  object.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  const rot = entity.rot || [0, 0, 0], scale = entity.scale || [1, 1, 1];
  const y = entity.pos[1];
  let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, minZ = Infinity, maxZ = -Infinity;
  for (const sx of [-0.5, 0.5]) for (const sz of [-0.5, 0.5]) {
    const p = new THREE.Vector3(sx, 0, sz).applyMatrix4(object.matrixWorld);
    minX = Math.min(minX, p.x); maxX = Math.max(maxX, p.x);
    minZ = Math.min(minZ, p.z); maxZ = Math.max(maxZ, p.z);
  }
  const fx = Math.sin(rot[1]), fz = Math.cos(rot[1]);
  const axis = Math.abs(fx) > Math.abs(fz) ? 'x' : 'z';
  const dir = (axis === 'x' ? fx : fz) >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
  return addRamp({
    minX, maxX, minZ, maxZ,
    startY: dir > 0 ? y : y + scale[1], endY: dir > 0 ? y + scale[1] : y, axis, dir
  });
}

export function createEntityObject(entity, { register = true } = {}) {
  if (RETIRED_ENTITY_IDS.has(entity.id)) return { object: null, record: null };
  const [x, y, z] = entity.pos;
  const rot = entity.rot || [0, 0, 0];
  const scale = entity.scale || [1, 1, 1];
  let object = null, record = null;

  switch (entity.type) {
    case 'vehicle': {
      const v = entity.vehicle || {};
      const cfg = {
        name: entity.name,
        bodyHex: colorToHex(entity.color || v.bodyHex, 0xdc2626),
        accentHex: colorToHex(v.accentHex, 0xf8fafc),
        x, z, yaw: rot[1], isCoupe: v.isCoupe !== false, isSupercar: !!v.isSupercar
      };
      if (register) { record = buildSportsCar(cfg); object = record.root; }
      else {
        object = buildSportsCarMesh(cfg).root;
        worldRoot.add(object);
        object.rotation.set(rot[0], rot[1], rot[2]);
      }
      object.position.y = y;
      if (scale[0] !== 1 || scale[1] !== 1 || scale[2] !== 1) object.scale.set(scale[0], scale[1], scale[2]);
      if (record) { record.y = y; record.carScale = object.scale.x; }
      if (!entity.color) applyModelPreset(v.isSupercar ? 'apex_gt' : 'coupe', object);
      break;
    }
    case 'pickup': {
      const p = entity.pickup || { kind: 'left', itemId: 'cash' };
      if (register) { record = spawnWorldPickup(p.kind, p.itemId, x, y, z, { rot, scale }); object = record.group; }
      else {
        object = buildPickupMesh(p.kind, p.itemId);
        object.position.set(x, y + ENTITY_Y_OFFSET.pickup, z);
        object.rotation.set(rot[0], rot[1], rot[2]);
        object.scale.set(scale[0], scale[1], scale[2]);
        worldRoot.add(object);
      }
      if (p.kind === 'right') applyModelPreset(p.itemId, object);
      break;
    }
    case 'crate':
      if (register) { record = spawnSmashCrate(x, y, z, entity.loot || null, { rot, scale }); object = record.mesh; }
      else {
        object = buildCrateMesh();
        object.position.set(x, y, z); object.rotation.set(rot[0], rot[1], rot[2]);
        object.scale.set(scale[0], scale[1], scale[2]); worldRoot.add(object);
      }
      break;
    case 'barrel':
      if (register) { record = spawnHazardBarrel(x, y, z, { rot, scale }); object = record.mesh; }
      else {
        object = buildBarrelMesh();
        object.position.set(x, y, z); object.rotation.set(rot[0], rot[1], rot[2]);
        object.scale.set(scale[0], scale[1], scale[2]); worldRoot.add(object);
      }
      break;
    case 'building':
      object = makeBuildingMesh(entity);
      object.position.set(x, y, z); object.rotation.set(rot[0], rot[1], rot[2]);
      object.scale.set(scale[0], scale[1], scale[2]); worldRoot.add(object);
      if (register) {
        object.updateMatrixWorld(true);
        const b = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(object);
        record = addSolidBox(b.min.x, b.max.x, b.min.y, b.max.y, b.min.z, b.max.z, 'entity_building', true);
      }
      break;
    case 'road':
      object = makeRoadMesh(entity);
      object.position.set(x, y, z); object.rotation.set(rot[0], rot[1], rot[2]);
      object.scale.set(scale[0], scale[1], scale[2]); worldRoot.add(object);
      if (register) record = registerRoadRamp(object, entity);
      break;
    case 'ramp':
      object = makeRampMesh(entity.color);
      object.position.set(x, y, z); object.rotation.set(rot[0], rot[1], rot[2]);
      object.scale.set(scale[0], scale[1], scale[2]); worldRoot.add(object);
      if (register) record = registerEntityRamp(object, entity);
      break;
    case 'block':
    case 'prop': {
      const shape = entity.type === 'block' ? 'box' : ((entity.prop && entity.prop.shape) || 'box');
      object = makePrimitiveMesh(shape, entity.color, entity.type === 'block' ? 0x94a3b8 : 0x38bdf8);
      object.position.set(x, y, z); object.rotation.set(rot[0], rot[1], rot[2]);
      object.scale.set(scale[0], scale[1], scale[2]); worldRoot.add(object);
      const solid = entity.type === 'block' || !entity.prop || entity.prop.solid !== false;
      if (register && solid) {
        object.updateMatrixWorld(true);
        const b = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(object);
        record = addSolidBox(b.min.x, b.max.x, b.min.y, b.max.y, b.min.z, b.max.z, 'entity_' + entity.type, true);
      }
      break;
    }
    default:
      console.warn('[worldRuntime] unknown entity type', entity.type);
      return { object: null, record: null };
  }

  applyEntityLook(object, entity);
  object.userData.studioName = object.userData.studioName || entity.name;
  tagEntity(object, entity.id);
  registerStudioObjectTree(object, `entity:${entity.id}`);
  entityObjects.set(entity.id, { entity, object, record });
  return { object, record };
}

export function spawnLevelEntities(data = levelData, opts = {}) {
  for (const e of data.entities) createEntityObject(e, opts);
}

export function removeEntityObject(id) {
  const rec = entityObjects.get(id);
  const sRec = studioObjects.get(id);
  if (!rec && !sRec) return;
  const targetObj = (rec && rec.object) || (sRec && (sRec.object || sRec));

  if (targetObj) {
    targetObj.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    targetObj.traverse((child) => {
      const i = sculptableTerrainMeshes.indexOf(child);
      if (i !== -1) sculptableTerrainMeshes.splice(i, 1);
    });
  }

  if (rec) {
    if (rec.record) {
      if ('minX' in rec.record && 'maxY' in rec.record) removeSolidBox(rec.record);
      else removeRamp(rec.record);
      for (const arr of [worldPickups, smashableCrates, hazardBarrels]) {
        const i = arr.indexOf(rec.record);
        if (i !== -1) arr.splice(i, 1);
      }
    }
    if (rec.entity && rec.entity.type === 'vehicle') {
      const vi = vehicles.findIndex((v) => v.root === rec.object);
      if (vi !== -1) vehicles.splice(vi, 1);
    }
    if (targetObj) {
      const bb = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(targetObj);
      if (!bb.isEmpty()) {
        const eps = 0.6;
        for (let i = worldBoxes.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
          const b = worldBoxes[i];
          if (!b.tag || !b.tag.startsWith('entity_')) continue;
          if (b.minX >= bb.min.x - eps && b.maxX <= bb.max.x + eps &&
              b.minZ >= bb.min.z - eps && b.maxZ <= bb.max.z + eps &&
              b.minY >= bb.min.y - eps && b.maxY <= bb.max.y + eps) removeSolidBox(b);
        }
      }
    }
    if (rec.object && rec.object.parent) rec.object.parent.remove(rec.object);
    for (const path of [...studioObjects.keys()]) {
      if (path === `entity:${id}` || path.startsWith(`entity:${id}/`)) {
        studioObjects.delete(path); studioBaseTransforms.delete(path);
      }
    }
    entityObjects.delete(id);
  }

  if (sRec) {
    const sObj = sRec.object || sRec;
    if (sObj && sObj.parent) {
      studioDeletedObjects.set(id, { object: sObj, parent: sObj.parent });
      sObj.parent.remove(sObj);
    }
    for (const path of [...studioObjects.keys()]) {
      if (path === id || path.startsWith(`${id}/`)) studioObjects.delete(path);
    }
  }
}

export function updateEntityTransform(entity) {
  const rec = entityObjects.get(entity.id);
  if (!rec || !rec.object) return;
  const o = rec.object;
  o.position.set(entity.pos[0], entity.pos[1] + (ENTITY_Y_OFFSET[entity.type] || 0), entity.pos[2]);
  o.rotation.set(entity.rot[0], entity.rot[1], entity.rot[2]);
  o.scale.set(entity.scale[0], entity.scale[1], entity.scale[2]);
  o.updateMatrixWorld(true);

  if (entity.type === 'vehicle') {
    rec.entity = entity;
    if ('carScale' in (rec.record || {})) rec.record.carScale = o.scale.x;
    const v = vehicles.find((c) => c.root === o);
    if (v) { v.x = entity.pos[0]; v.y = entity.pos[1]; v.z = entity.pos[2]; v.yaw = entity.rot[1]; v.carScale = o.scale.x; }
    return;
  }
  if (entity.type === 'road' || entity.type === 'ramp') {
    if (rec.record) {
      if ('minX' in rec.record && 'maxY' in rec.record) removeSolidBox(rec.record);
      else removeRamp(rec.record);
    }
    rec.record = entity.type === 'road' ? registerRoadRamp(o, entity) : registerEntityRamp(o, entity);
    rec.entity = entity;
    return;
  }
  if (rec.record && 'minX' in rec.record && 'maxY' in rec.record) {
    const bb = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(o);
    // Mutate in place, then re-index: the box may have moved cells.
    removeSolidBox(rec.record);
    rec.record = addSolidBox(bb.min.x, bb.max.x, bb.min.y, bb.max.y, bb.min.z, bb.max.z,
      rec.record.tag, rec.record.climbable);
  }
  rec.entity = entity;
}

// =============================================================================
// SECTION 11 â€” FRAME TICK & WATER HOOK
// =============================================================================
function updateWaterOnce(dt, elapsed) {
  if (elapsed === lastWaterFrame) return;
  lastWaterFrame = elapsed;
  updateWater(dt, elapsed);
}

/** ONE call per frame. Replaces the four separate update entry points. */
export function updateWorldFrame(dt, elapsed) {
  updateWorldStream();
  updateLampposts(dt, elapsed);
  updateWorldSystems(dt, elapsed);
  updateEdgeBarrier();
  updateRiverFlow(dt);
  updateWaterOnce(dt, elapsed);
}

export function revealAllLods() {
  revealAllCells();
  revealEdgeBarrier();
}
export function restoreAllLods() { restoreCulling(); }
