---js\worldStream.js start---

// =============================================================================
// worldStream.js â€” ONE distance/height streamer for the whole world.
//
// Replaces three separate systems:
//   â€¢ registerBuildingLod / updateBuildingLods   (visibility LOD)
//   â€¢ aura.culls                                (visibility culls)
//   â€¢ updateSkyscraperInteriors                 (lobby load/unload + colliders)
//
// Adds:
//   â€¢ LAZY prefab compilation â€” a cell's geometry does not exist until you
//     walk near it, and is cached after the first visit.
//   â€¢ VERTICAL bands â€” a 24-floor tower only pays for the floor you are on
//     plus its neighbours, no matter how many floors exist.
//   â€¢ Bucketed cell index for loads, an ACTIVE SET for unloads. Both are
//     required: a cell you walked away from is no longer in your bucket, so
//     scanning only the bucket would never unload it.
//   â€¢ Load/unload budget with urgent bypass, so streaming never hitches and
//     never drops the floor out from under the player.
//
// CELL MODES
//   'stream'  group parented on load, detached on unload; colliders follow.
//   'lod'     group stays parented; only .visible toggles (cheap).
//   'inverse' visible when FAR (distant shell tier). Swept separately, because
//             a far cell is by definition never in the player's bucket.
//
// COLLIDER OWNERSHIP
//   A 'stream' cell handed `boxes` TAKES OWNERSHIP of them. They are removed
//   from worldBoxes at registration and re-added on every activate(). This is
//   what prevents the double-registration bug when worldKit hands over an
//   interior it already built.
// =============================================================================
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { aura, buildingLods, hooks, player, skyscrapers } from './state.js';
import { worldRoot } from './engine.js';
import {
  addSolidBox, compilePrefab, dist2, getStreamCells, removeSolidBoxes, rngFor
} from './worldKit.js';

const CELL = 96;                        // spatial bucket size (metres)
const buckets = new Map();              // bucketKey -> cell[]
const cells = new Map();                // id -> cell
const active = new Set();               // every loaded cell (unload sweep)
const inverse = new Set();              // far-tier cells (distance toggle)

let opts = {
  loadPerFrame: 3,
  unloadPerFrame: 6,
  urgentRadius: 14,
  defaultOn: 54,
  defaultOff: 66,
  parent: worldRoot
};

let revealAll = false;
let stats = { loads: 0, unloads: 0, compiled: 0, active: 0 };

const bucketKey = (cx, cz) => (cx + 32768) * 65536 + (cz + 32768);

function indexCell(c) {
  const reach = Math.max(c.r, c.off || 0);
  const cover = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(reach / CELL));
  const cx = Math.floor(c.x / CELL), cz = Math.floor(c.z / CELL);
  c._keys = [];
  for (let ix = cx - cover; ix <= cx + cover; ix++) {
    for (let iz = cz - cover; iz <= cz + cover; iz++) {
      const k = bucketKey(ix, iz);
      let arr = buckets.get(k);
      if (!arr) { arr = []; buckets.set(k, arr); }
      arr.push(c);
      c._keys.push(k);
    }
  }
}

function unindexCell(c) {
  for (const k of c._keys || []) {
    const arr = buckets.get(k);
    if (!arr) continue;
    const i = arr.indexOf(c);
    if (i !== -1) arr.splice(i, 1);
    if (!arr.length) buckets.delete(k);
  }
  c._keys = null;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Registration
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function initWorldStream(o = {}) {
  opts = { ...opts, ...o };
  hooks.worldStreamRegister = registerCell;
  return opts;
}

/**
 * cell = {
 *   id, x, y, z, r, off?, band?, mode?, tier?, priority?,
 *   group?, boxes?,                 // already-built content
 *   prefab?, w?, d?, ry?, s?,       // lazy-compiled content
 *   legacy?: 'skyscraper', sk?, makeInterior?
 * }
 */
export function registerCell(cell) {
  if (!cell || !cell.id) return null;
  if (cells.has(cell.id)) unregisterCell(cell.id);

  const c = {
    y: 0, band: 0, off: 0, active: false, cached: false,
    boxes: [], group: null, priority: 0, ...cell
  };
  if (!c.mode) c.mode = (c.prefab || c.legacy) ? 'stream' : 'lod';
  if (c.tier === 'far') c.mode = 'inverse';
  c.r = Number.isFinite(c.r) ? c.r : opts.defaultOn;
  c.off = Number.isFinite(c.off) && c.off > c.r ? c.off : Math.max(c.r * 1.22, c.r + 12);

  // Take ownership of handed-over colliders so activate() never double-adds.
  if (c.mode === 'stream' && c.boxes.length) {
    c._pendingSolids = c.boxes.map((b) => ({
      b: [b.minX, b.maxX, b.minY, b.maxY, b.minZ, b.maxZ], tag: b.tag, climb: b.climbable
    }));
    removeSolidBoxes(c.boxes);
    if (c.group && c.group.parent) c.group.parent.remove(c.group);
  }

  cells.set(c.id, c);
  if (c.mode === 'inverse') {
    inverse.add(c);
    c.active = true;
    if (c.group) c.group.visible = true;
  } else {
    indexCell(c);
  }
  return c;
}

export function unregisterCell(id) {
  const c = cells.get(id);
  if (!c) return;
  if (c.active) deactivate(c, true);
  unindexCell(c);
  inverse.delete(c);
  active.delete(c);
  cells.delete(id);
}

/** Pull in everything worldKit queued during the static build. */
export function adoptKitCells() {
  let n = 0;
  for (const c of getStreamCells()) if (registerCell(c)) n++;
  return n;
}

/** Legacy: registerBuildingLod() entries -> 'lod' cells (height-aware radius). */
export function adoptBuildingLods() {
  let n = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < buildingLods.length; i++) {
    const e = buildingLods[i];
    const r = Number.isFinite(e.r) ? e.r : 54;
    const off = Number.isFinite(e.off) ? e.off : Math.max(66, r * 1.22);
    if (registerCell({
      id: `lod:${i}`, x: e.x, z: e.z, group: e.group,
      mode: 'lod', r, off, legacyRef: e
    })) n++;
  }
  return n;
}

/** Legacy: aura.culls entries -> 'lod' cells. */
export function adoptAuraCulls() {
  let n = 0;
  const list = aura.culls || [];
  for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
    const c = list[i];
    if (registerCell({
      id: `cull:${i}`, x: c.x, z: c.z, group: c.g,
      mode: 'lod', r: c.r, off: c.r * 1.18, legacyRef: c
    })) n++;
  }
  return n;
}

/** Legacy: skyscraper lobbies keep their maker fn, but stream through here. */
export function adoptSkyscraperInteriors(makeInterior) {
  let n = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < skyscrapers.length; i++) {
    const sk = skyscrapers[i];
    if (registerCell({
      id: `sk:${i}:lobby`, x: sk.x, z: sk.z, y: sk.baseY ?? 0.18,
      r: 56, off: 70, mode: 'stream', legacy: 'skyscraper',
      sk, makeInterior, tier: 'interior'
    })) n++;
  }
  return n;
}

/** Register the furnished floors produced by expandTowerFloors(). */
export function registerTowerFloors(rec, row, meta, expand) {
  const list = expand(rec, row, meta);
  for (const c of list) registerCell(c);
  rec.floorCells = list.map((c) => c.id);
  return list.length;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Activation
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
function buildFromPrefab(c) {
  const out = { parts: [], solids: [], anchors: {}, nested: [] };
  const xf = { x: c.x, y: c.y, z: c.z, ry: c.ry || 0, s: c.s || 1 };
  // Parametric prefabs (tower floors) receive their dimensions through the row.
  compilePrefab(c.prefab, xf, out, rngFor(c.id), 0, true,
    { w: c.w, d: c.d, ry: c.ry, s: c.s });

  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.userData.studioName = `stream:${c.id}`;
  g.userData.studioSelectable = false;
  g.userData.worldBuilt = true;
  for (const p of out.parts) {
    const m = new THREE.Mesh(p.geometry, p.material);
    m.position.set(p.x, p.y, p.z);
    m.scale.set(p.sx, p.sy, p.sz);
    m.rotation.set(p.rx, p.ry, p.rz);
    m.castShadow = !!p.cast;
    m.receiveShadow = !!p.recv;
    if (p.name) m.userData.studioName = p.name;
    g.add(m);
  }
  c.group = g;
  c._pendingSolids = out.solids;
  c.anchors = out.anchors;
  stats.compiled++;
  return g;
}

function activate(c) {
  if (c.active) return;

  if (c.mode === 'lod' || c.mode === 'inverse') {
    if (c.group) c.group.visible = true;
    if (c.legacyRef) c.legacyRef.active = true;
    c.active = true;
    active.add(c);
    stats.loads++;
    return;
  }

  // --- legacy skyscraper lobby --------------------------------------------
  if (c.legacy === 'skyscraper') {
    const sk = c.sk;
    const baseY = sk.baseY ?? 0.18;
    let ig = sk.interiorCache;
    if (!ig) {
      ig = new THREE.Group();
      ig.position.set(sk.x, baseY, sk.z);
      ig.userData.studioSelectable = false;
      sk.interiorSpecs = c.makeInterior ? c.makeInterior(sk, ig) : [];
      sk.interiorCache = ig;
    }
    opts.parent.add(ig);
    sk.interiorGroup = ig;
    sk.interiorBoxes = (sk.interiorSpecs || []).map((b) =>
      addSolidBox(b.minX, b.maxX, b.minY, b.maxY, b.minZ, b.maxZ, b.tag, b.climbable));
    sk.interiorLoaded = true;
    c.group = ig;
    c.boxes = sk.interiorBoxes;
    c.cached = true;
    c.active = true;
    active.add(c);
    stats.loads++;
    return;
  }

  // --- lazily compiled / cached prefab content -----------------------------
  if (!c.group) buildFromPrefab(c);
  if (c.group && !c.group.parent) opts.parent.add(c.group);
  if (c.group) c.group.visible = true;

  if (c._pendingSolids) {
    c.boxes = c._pendingSolids.map((s) =>
      addSolidBox(s.b[0], s.b[1], s.b[2], s.b[3], s.b[4], s.b[5], s.tag, s.climb));
    c._pendingSolids = null;
  } else if (c.boxes.length) {
    // Re-register cached colliders (they were spliced on unload).
    c.boxes = c.boxes.map((b) =>
      addSolidBox(b.minX, b.maxX, b.minY, b.maxY, b.minZ, b.maxZ, b.tag, b.climbable));
  }

  c.cached = true;
  c.active = true;
  active.add(c);
  stats.loads++;
}

function deactivate(c, destroy = false) {
  if (!c.active && !destroy) return;

  if (c.mode === 'lod' || c.mode === 'inverse') {
    if (c.group) c.group.visible = false;
    if (c.legacyRef) c.legacyRef.active = false;
    c.active = false;
    active.delete(c);
    stats.unloads++;
    return;
  }

  if (c.legacy === 'skyscraper') {
    const sk = c.sk;
    if (sk.interiorGroup) { opts.parent.remove(sk.interiorGroup); sk.interiorGroup = null; }
    removeSolidBoxes(sk.interiorBoxes);
    sk.interiorBoxes = [];
    sk.interiorLoaded = false;
    c.boxes = [];
    if (destroy) { sk.interiorCache = null; sk.interiorSpecs = null; c.group = null; c.cached = false; }
  } else {
    if (c.group && c.group.parent) c.group.parent.remove(c.group);
    removeSolidBoxes(c.boxes);
    if (destroy && c.group) { c.group = null; c.cached = false; c._pendingSolids = null; }
  }

  c.active = false;
  active.delete(c);
  stats.unloads++;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Per-frame update
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
function wantsActive(c, d2) {
  if (revealAll) return true;
  let inRange = c.active ? d2 < c.off * c.off : d2 < c.r * c.r;
  if (c.mode === 'inverse') inRange = !inRange;
  if (!inRange) return false;
  if (c.band > 0) {
    const dy = Math.abs((player.y || 0) - c.y);
    return dy <= (c.active ? c.band * 1.4 : c.band);
  }
  return true;
}

export function updateWorldStream() {
  const px = player.x, pz = player.z;

  // 1. UNLOAD SWEEP â€” O(active), independent of bucket membership. Without
  //    this, anything you walk away from stays resident forever.
  let unloaded = 0;
  for (const c of active) {
    const d2 = dist2(px, pz, c.x, c.z);
    if (wantsActive(c, d2)) continue;
    // Budget, with a hard override for cells that are already far outside.
    if (unloaded >= opts.unloadPerFrame && d2 < c.off * c.off * 4) continue;
    deactivate(c);
    unloaded++;
  }

  // 2. FAR-TIER SHELLS â€” visible when distant, hidden when you approach.
  for (const c of inverse) {
    const want = revealAll || dist2(px, pz, c.x, c.z) >= c.r * c.r;
    if (want === c.active) continue;
    c.active = want;
    if (c.group) c.group.visible = want;
    if (c.legacyRef) c.legacyRef.active = want;
  }

  // 3. LOAD CANDIDATES â€” only the player's bucket.
  const arr = buckets.get(bucketKey(Math.floor(px / CELL), Math.floor(pz / CELL)));
  if (arr && arr.length) {
    const toLoad = [];
    const urgent2 = opts.urgentRadius * opts.urgentRadius;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
      const c = arr[i];
      if (c.active) continue;
      const d2 = dist2(px, pz, c.x, c.z);
      if (!wantsActive(c, d2)) continue;
      // Urgent bypass: never make the player wait for the floor they're on.
      if (d2 < urgent2 && (c.band === 0 || Math.abs((player.y || 0) - c.y) <= c.band)) {
        activate(c);
      } else {
        c._d2 = d2;
        toLoad.push(c);
      }
    }
    if (toLoad.length > 1) {
      toLoad.sort((a, b) => (b.priority - a.priority) || (a._d2 - b._d2));
    }
    const n = Math.min(opts.loadPerFrame, toLoad.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) activate(toLoad[i]);
  }

  stats.active = active.size;
  return stats;
}

/** Force-load a cell NOW. Call before teleporting the player onto it. */
export function primeCell(id) {
  const c = cells.get(id);
  if (c && !c.active) activate(c);
  return c || null;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Studio / debug / teardown
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function revealAllCells() {
  revealAll = true;
  for (const c of cells.values()) if (!c.active) activate(c);
}

export function restoreCulling() {
  revealAll = false;
  for (const c of cells.values()) {
    if (c.mode === 'inverse') continue;
    if (!wantsActive(c, dist2(player.x, player.z, c.x, c.z))) deactivate(c);
  }
}

export function streamStats() {
  return {
    ...stats,
    cells: cells.size,
    buckets: buckets.size,
    inverse: inverse.size,
    revealAll
  };
}

export function getCell(id) { return cells.get(id) || null; }
export function activeCells() { return [...active]; }

export function resetWorldStream() {
  for (const c of cells.values()) {
    if (c.active) deactivate(c, true);
    if (c.group && c.group.parent) c.group.parent.remove(c.group);
  }
  cells.clear();
  buckets.clear();
  active.clear();
  inverse.clear();
  stats = { loads: 0, unloads: 0, compiled: 0, active: 0 };
  revealAll = false;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Back-compatible entry points â€” old callers keep working, one system runs.
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function updateBuildingLods() { /* handled by updateWorldStream() */ }
export function updateSkyscraperInteriors() { /* handled by updateWorldStream() */ }
export function updateAuraCulls() { /* handled by updateWorldStream() */ }

export default {
  initWorldStream, registerCell, unregisterCell, updateWorldStream, primeCell,
  adoptKitCells, adoptBuildingLods, adoptAuraCulls, adoptSkyscraperInteriors,
  registerTowerFloors, revealAllCells, restoreCulling, resetWorldStream,
  streamStats, getCell, activeCells
};

---js\worldStream.js end---


---js\worldObjects\airport.js start---

// =============================================================================
// worldObjects/airport.js â€” LAX TERMINAL (parametric barrel-vault airport)
//
// ONE GROUP at (TX, Y, TZ). All geometry is LOCAL; colliders are offset back
// to world. Move the row and the whole airport moves â€” including gate pods,
// boarding bridges and the mezzanine stair.
//
// REMOVED vs the original (now separate layout rows â€” would double-build):
//   curb lane + dashes + zebras -> rd/lax_curb      (worldRoads)
//   bag carts / tug / masts / cones -> lax/cart_*, lax/tug, lax/mast_*, lax/cones
//
// PUBLISHED for worldSystems/airport.js via getPlacement(id).data:
//   board    { canvas, tex, rows, draw() }   departures board, refreshable
//   carousel THREE.Group                     baggage claim, spins
// =============================================================================
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { aura, enterableZones, mapShapes } from '../state.js';
import { makeMat, worldRoot } from '../engine.js';
import { UNIT_BOX, addSolidBox, flushInstances, makeBox, rngFor, xform } from '../worldKit.js';
import { neon } from './shell.js';

const ownedMats = [];
const own = (m) => { m.userData.shared = true; ownedMats.push(m); return m; };

/** Barrel-vault surface in local space. Reused for shell + skylight. */
function archShell(halfSpan, length, baseY, rise, segX = 28, t0 = 0, t1 = 1) {
  const pos = [], uv = [], idx = [];
  for (let j = 0; j <= 1; j++) {
    const z = -length / 2 + j * length;
    for (let i = 0; i <= segX; i++) {
      const t = t0 + (t1 - t0) * (i / segX);
      pos.push(-halfSpan + t * 2 * halfSpan, baseY + rise * Math.sin(Math.PI * t), z);
      uv.push(t * 8, j * length / 12);
    }
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < segX; i++) {
    const a = i, b = i + 1, c = i + segX + 1, d = i + segX + 2;
    idx.push(a, c, b, b, c, d);
  }
  const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
  g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uv, 2));
  g.setIndex(idx);
  g.computeVertexNormals();
  return g;
}

/** Departures board â€” geometry module creates it, airport system refreshes it. */
export function makeDeparturesBoard(cfg = {}, seed = 'lax/board') {
  const rng = rngFor(seed);
  const dests = cfg.dests || ['SUNNYVALE', 'AURA HILLS', 'REDROCK', 'FROSTPINE'];
  const statuses = cfg.statuses || ['ON TIME', 'BOARDING', 'DELAYED'];
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = 512; canvas.height = 320;
  const rows = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < (cfg.boardRows ?? 7); i++) {
    const hh = 9 + Math.floor(rng() * 12), mm = Math.floor(rng() * 6) * 10;
    rows.push({
      code: `AU ${100 + Math.floor(rng() * 800)}`,
      dest: dests[i % dests.length],
      time: `${String(hh).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(mm).padStart(2, '0')}`,
      status: statuses[Math.floor(rng() * statuses.length)]
    });
  }
  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  const draw = () => {
    const c = canvas.getContext('2d');
    c.fillStyle = '#05070d'; c.fillRect(0, 0, 512, 320);
    c.textAlign = 'left';
    c.fillStyle = '#facc15'; c.font = 'bold 30px Oswald, sans-serif';
    c.fillText('DEPARTURES', 18, 38);
    c.fillStyle = '#38bdf8'; c.font = 'bold 17px Oswald, sans-serif';
    c.fillText('FLIGHT', 18, 68); c.fillText('DESTINATION', 120, 68);
    c.fillText('TIME', 330, 68); c.fillText('STATUS', 410, 68);
    c.strokeStyle = '#1e3a5f'; c.lineWidth = 2;
    c.beginPath(); c.moveTo(14, 78); c.lineTo(498, 78); c.stroke();
    c.font = '18px Oswald, sans-serif';
    rows.forEach((r, i) => {
      const y = 106 + i * 30;
      c.fillStyle = '#e2e8f0';
      c.fillText(r.code, 18, y); c.fillText(r.dest, 120, y); c.fillText(r.time, 330, y);
      c.fillStyle = r.status === 'DELAYED' ? '#f87171'
        : (r.status === 'BOARDING' || r.status === 'FINAL CALL' ? '#4ade80' : '#facc15');
      c.fillText(r.status, 410, y);
    });
    tex.needsUpdate = true;
  };
  draw();
  return { canvas, tex, rows, draw, statuses };
}

// =============================================================================
export function buildAirportTerminal(row) {
  const TX = row.at[0], TZ = row.at[1];
  const HW = row.halfW ?? 22, HL = row.halfL ?? 103;
  const Y = row.baseY ?? 0.30, WALL_H = row.wallH ?? 14.0, RISE = row.rise ?? 6.4;
  const MEZZ = (row.mezz ?? 7.60) - Y;      // local deck height
  const TOP = WALL_H;                       // local wall top
  const DECK = 2.15;                        // local boarding-bridge deck
  const L = (absZ) => absZ - TZ;            // absolute layout z -> local
  const doors = (row.doors || [TZ - 65, TZ, TZ + 65]).map(L);
  const gates = (row.gates || [{ z: TZ - 63, n: 1 }, { z: TZ + 57, n: 2 }])
    .map((g) => ({ z: L(g.z), n: g.n }));
  const checkin = (row.checkin || [TZ - 73, TZ - 41, TZ + 41, TZ + 73]).map(L);
  const security = (row.security || [TZ - 14, TZ, TZ + 14]).map(L);

  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.position.set(TX, Y, TZ);
  g.userData.studioName = row.name || 'LAX Terminal 1';
  worldRoot.add(g);

  const boxes = [];
  const B = (p, w, h, d, x, y, z, m, o) => makeBox(p, w, h, d, x, y, z, m, o);
  const S = (x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1, tag, climb) => boxes.push(addSolidBox(
    TX + x0, TX + x1, Y + y0, Y + y1, TZ + z0, TZ + z1, tag, climb));

  // --- materials -----------------------------------------------------------
  const shellMat = own(makeMat(0xeef4f8, { texType: 'brushedsteel', repeatX: 10, repeatY: 30, metalness: 0.72, roughness: 0.26, flatShading: false }));
  const ribMat = own(makeMat(0xcbd5e1, { texType: 'brushedsteel', metalness: 0.92, roughness: 0.2, flatShading: false }));
  const mullion = own(makeMat(0x475569, { metalness: 0.8, roughness: 0.35 }));
  const curtain = own(makeMat(0x9fd8f5, { metalness: 0.85, roughness: 0.06, transparent: true, opacity: 0.26, emissive: 0x0ea5e9, emissiveIntensity: 0.22, flatShading: false }));
  curtain.side = THREE.DoubleSide; curtain.depthWrite = false;
  const floorMat = own(makeMat(0xf1f5f9, { texType: 'marble', repeatX: 12, repeatY: 52, roughness: 0.18, clearcoat: 0.8, flatShading: false }));
  const white = own(makeMat(0xf8fafc, { roughness: 0.55 }));
  const deskMat = own(makeMat(0x1e293b, { metalness: 0.4, roughness: 0.4 }));
  const topMat = own(makeMat(0xe2e8f0, { texType: 'brushedsteel', metalness: 0.9, roughness: 0.2, flatShading: false }));
  const seatMat = own(makeMat(0x2563eb, { texType: 'fabric', roughness: 0.9 }));
  const beltMat = own(makeMat(0x334155, { metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.5 }));
  const woodMat = own(makeMat(0x8a5a2b, { texType: 'wood', repeatX: 4, repeatY: 4, roughness: 0.6 }));
  const glowMat = own(makeMat(0xfff7d6, { emissive: 0xfff7d6, emissiveIntensity: 0.9 }));
  const brandMat = own(makeMat(0x0284c7, { emissive: 0x0369a1, emissiveIntensity: 0.45, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.3 }));
  const paint = own(new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfacc15 }));

  // --- podium, floor, guide bands -----------------------------------------
  B(g, 2 * HW + 2.4, 0.30, 2 * HL + 2.4, 0, -0.15, 0,
    own(makeMat(0xcbd5e1, { texType: 'concrete', repeatX: 12, repeatY: 52 })), { recv: true });
  B(g, 2 * HW - 0.4, 0.10, 2 * HL - 0.4, 0, 0.05, 0, floorMat, { recv: true, name: 'Terminal floor' });
  S(-HW, HW, -0.30, 0.10, -HL, HL, 'terminal_floor', true);
  for (const bx of [-14, 0, 14]) B(g, 0.5, 0.02, 2 * HL - 6, bx, 0.11, 0, brandMat);

  // --- barrel vault + ribs + skylight --------------------------------------
  const shell = new THREE.Mesh(archShell(HW + 1.2, 2 * HL + 2.4, TOP, RISE), shellMat);
  shell.castShadow = shell.receiveShadow = true;
  shell.material.side = THREE.DoubleSide;
  shell.userData.studioName = 'Terminal vault roof';
  g.add(shell);
  const ribPts = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= 24; i++) {
    const t = i / 24;
    ribPts.push(new THREE.Vector3(-(HW + 1.2) + t * 2 * (HW + 1.2), TOP + RISE * Math.sin(Math.PI * t) - 0.34, 0));
  }
  const ribGeo = new THREE.TubeGeometry(new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(ribPts), 26, 0.30, 6, false);
  const ribT = [];
  for (let z = -HL - 1; z <= HL + 1; z += 11.5) xform(ribT, 0, 0, z);
  flushInstances(g, ribGeo, ribMat, ribT, { cast: true });
  const sky = new THREE.Mesh(archShell(HW + 1.2, 2 * HL - 4, TOP, RISE, 10, 0.40, 0.60),
    own(makeMat(0xdff3ff, { emissive: 0xbfe6ff, emissiveIntensity: 0.55, transparent: true, opacity: 0.55, roughness: 0.1 })));
  sky.material.side = THREE.DoubleSide;
  sky.position.y = 0.12;
  g.add(sky);
  for (const s of [-1, 1]) B(g, 0.6, 0.9, 2 * HL + 2.4, s * (HW + 1.2), TOP + 0.1, 0, brandMat);
  S(-HW - 1.2, HW + 1.2, TOP, TOP + RISE + 0.4, -HL - 1.2, HL + 1.2, 'terminal_roof', true);

  const colT = [];
  for (let z = -HL + 6; z <= HL - 6; z += 17) for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
    xform(colT, s * (HW - 1.4), WALL_H / 2, z, 0.9, WALL_H, 0.9);
  }
  flushInstances(g, UNIT_BOX, ribMat, colT, { cast: true, recv: true });

  // --- curtain walls -------------------------------------------------------
  const wallRun = (lx, a, b, outward) => {
    if (b - a < 0.4) return;
    B(g, 0.5, WALL_H, b - a, lx, WALL_H / 2, (a + b) / 2, white);
    S(lx - 0.25, lx + 0.25, 0, TOP, a, b, 'terminal_wall', false);
    B(g, 0.16, WALL_H - 1.2, b - a - 0.6, lx + outward * 0.30, WALL_H / 2, (a + b) / 2, curtain, { order: 3 })
      .userData.studioName = 'Curtain wall';
    const mT = [];
    for (let z = a + 1.2; z < b - 0.6; z += 2.4) xform(mT, lx + outward * 0.36, WALL_H / 2, z, 0.16, WALL_H - 1.2, 0.16);
    for (const hy of [3.4, 7.0, 10.6]) xform(mT, lx + outward * 0.36, hy, (a + b) / 2, 0.16, 0.16, b - a - 0.6);
    flushInstances(g, UNIT_BOX, mullion, mT);
  };
  // Landside (west): openings at the entrance portals.
  {
    let cursor = -HL;
    for (const dz of doors) { wallRun(-HW, cursor, dz - 4, -1); cursor = dz + 4; }
    wallRun(-HW, cursor, HL, -1);
    for (const dz of doors) {
      B(g, 0.5, WALL_H - 4.6, 8, -HW, 4.6 + (WALL_H - 4.6) / 2, dz, white);
      B(g, 0.9, 0.34, 8.6, -HW - 0.2, 4.6, dz, ribMat);
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) B(g, 0.2, 4.4, 0.3, -HW - 0.2, 2.2, dz + s * 4, ribMat);
      neon(g, dz === doors[1] ? 'DEPARTURES  â€¢  TERMINAL 1' : 'ENTRANCE',
        -HW - 0.62, 5.5, dz, '#090d14', dz === doors[1] ? '#facc15' : '#38bdf8',
        dz === doors[1] ? 9.0 : 4.4, 0.9);
    }
  }
  // Airside (east): openings at the gate pods.
  {
    let cursor = -HL;
    for (const gt of gates) { wallRun(HW, cursor, gt.z - 8, 1); cursor = gt.z + 8; }
    wallRun(HW, cursor, HL, 1);
  }
  for (const ez of [-1, 1]) {
    const wz = ez * HL;
    B(g, 2 * HW, WALL_H, 0.5, 0, WALL_H / 2, wz, white);
    S(-HW, HW, 0, TOP, wz - 0.25, wz + 0.25, 'terminal_wall', false);
    B(g, 2 * HW - 5, 5.0, 0.2, 0, 8.0, wz + ez * 0.32, curtain, { order: 3 });
  }

  // --- curbside canopy (the lane itself is rd/lax_curb) --------------------
  const canZ = HL - 24;
  B(g, 19, 0.55, canZ * 2, -29, 7.05, 0, shellMat, { cast: true, recv: true, name: 'Departures canopy' });
  B(g, 19.4, 0.35, canZ * 2 + 0.6, -29, 6.72, 0, brandMat);
  S(-38.5, -19.5, 6.70, 7.35, -canZ, canZ, 'curb_canopy', true);
  for (let z = -canZ + 6; z <= canZ - 6; z += 24.3) {
    const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.42, 0.52, 7.0, 10), ribMat);
    c.position.set(-36, 3.2, z); c.castShadow = true; g.add(c);
    S(-36.5, -35.5, -0.04, 6.70, z - 0.5, z + 0.5, 'canopy_column', false);
  }
  const stripT = [];
  for (let z = -canZ + 4; z <= canZ - 4; z += 10) xform(stripT, -29, 6.62, z, 12, 0.1, 0.6);
  flushInstances(g, UNIT_BOX, glowMat, stripT);
  neon(g, 'LOS AURA INTERNATIONAL', -38.8, 8.6, 0, '#090d14', '#facc15', 22.0, 2.0);
  neon(g, 'LAX  â€¢  TERMINAL 1  â€¢  DEPARTURES', -38.8, 5.9, 0, '#0b1220', '#38bdf8', 17.0, 1.0);

  // =========================================================================
  // INTERIOR (distance-culled)
  // =========================================================================
  const inn = new THREE.Group();
  inn.userData.studioName = 'LAX terminal interior';
  g.add(inn);
  const I = (w, h, d, x, y, z, m, o) => makeBox(inn, w, h, d, x, y, z, m, o);

  // Check-in islands
  const screenT = [], beltT = [];
  checkin.forEach((cz, i) => {
    I(5.0, 1.15, 16.0, -9, 0.575, cz, deskMat, { cast: true });
    I(5.3, 0.12, 16.3, -9, 1.21, cz, topMat);
    I(0.6, 1.9, 16.0, -6.2, 2.1, cz, deskMat);
    S(-11.5, -6.5, 0, 1.27, cz - 8, cz + 8, 'checkin_counter', true);
    for (let k = -3; k <= 3; k++) {
      xform(screenT, -6.6, 2.35, cz + k * 2.2, 0.12, 0.85, 1.5);
      xform(beltT, -12.6, 0.42, cz + k * 2.2, 1.6, 0.5, 1.7);
    }
    I(6.0, 0.12, 1.2, -9, 3.6, cz, brandMat);
    neon(inn, `CHECK-IN ${cz < 0 ? 'A' : 'B'}${i + 1}`, -9, 3.05, cz, '#0b1220', '#38bdf8', 4.6, 0.62);
  });
  flushInstances(inn, UNIT_BOX, own(makeMat(0x22d3ee, { emissive: 0x0891b2, emissiveIntensity: 0.8 })), screenT);
  flushInstances(inn, UNIT_BOX, beltMat, beltT, { recv: true });
  neon(inn, 'CHECK-IN  â€¢  DESKS 1â€“28', -9, 5.6, 0, '#0b1220', '#38bdf8', 13.0, 1.0);

  // Hanging departures board (landside + airside faces)
  const board = makeDeparturesBoard(row.flights || {}, row.id);
  const boardMat = own(new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    map: board.tex, emissiveMap: board.tex, emissive: 0xffffff, emissiveIntensity: 0.9, roughness: 0.3
  }));
  const faces = [boardMat, boardMat, deskMat, deskMat, deskMat, deskMat];
  I(0.5, 10.0, 16.0, -2.5, 9.4, 0, faces, { name: 'Departures board' });
  I(0.5, 7.5, 12.0, 8.0, 8.4, 0, faces);
  for (const bx of [-2.5, 8.0]) for (const s of [-1, 1]) I(0.16, 3.2, 0.16, bx, 15.0, s * 5.5, ribMat);

  // Security checkpoint
  const secLo = security[0] - 6, secHi = security[security.length - 1] + 6;
  for (const [a, b] of [[-HL + 0.5, secLo], [secHi, HL - 0.5]]) {
    I(0.6, 6.0, b - a, 2, 3.0, (a + b) / 2, own(makeMat(0xe2e8f0, { texType: 'siding', repeatX: 2, repeatY: 20 })));
    S(1.7, 2.3, 0, 6.0, a, b, 'security_divider', false);
    I(0.2, 3.4, b - a - 1, 2.35, 4.0, (a + b) / 2, curtain, { order: 3 });
  }
  for (const lz of security) {
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
      I(0.45, 3.0, 4.4, 2 + sx * 2.0, 1.5, lz, ribMat, { cast: true });
      S(2 + sx * 2.0 - 0.23, 2 + sx * 2.0 + 0.23, 0, 3.0, lz - 2.2, lz + 2.2, 'security_arch', false);
    }
    I(4.6, 0.35, 4.4, 2, 3.15, lz, ribMat);
    I(1.9, 0.85, 6.0, -1.0, 0.43, lz, beltMat, { cast: true });
    I(2.2, 1.35, 2.4, -1.0, 1.35, lz + 1.9, deskMat, { cast: true });
    S(-2.0, 0.0, 0, 0.86, lz - 3, lz + 3, 'security_belt', true);
    const binT = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) xform(binT, -1.8, 0.95, lz - 2.2 + i * 0.8, 1.3, 0.22, 0.6);
    flushInstances(inn, UNIT_BOX, own(makeMat(0x64748b, { roughness: 0.7 })), binT);
  }
  neon(inn, 'SECURITY  â†’  GATES 1â€“8', 2, 5.4, secLo - 2, '#065f46', '#f8fafc', 11.0, 0.9);
  const stanchT = [];
  for (let z = secLo + 1; z <= secHi - 1; z += 3.2) for (const sx of [-4.2, 4.2]) xform(stanchT, 2 + sx, 0.5, z, 0.1, 1.0, 0.1);
  flushInstances(inn, UNIT_BOX, ribMat, stanchT);

  // Public mezzanine
  const mz = HL - 8;
  I(13.6, 0.30, mz * 2, -15.0, MEZZ - 0.15, 0, floorMat, { recv: true });
  S(-21.8, -8.2, MEZZ - 0.30, MEZZ, -mz, mz, 'mezzanine_deck', true);
  const stairTop = 31, stairBot = 47;
  for (const [a, b] of [[-mz, stairTop], [stairBot, mz]]) {
    I(0.16, 1.15, b - a, -8.2, MEZZ + 0.575, (a + b) / 2, ribMat);
    I(0.10, 0.95, b - a - 0.4, -8.2, MEZZ + 0.55, (a + b) / 2, curtain, { order: 3 });
    S(-8.4, -8.0, MEZZ, MEZZ + 1.15, a, b, 'mezz_rail', true);
  }
  const stairMat = own(makeMat(0xd8dee6, { texType: 'concrete', repeatX: 2, repeatY: 1 }));
  for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) {
    const sy = 0.10 + i * 0.52, sz = stairBot - 1 - i * 1.1;
    I(4.4, 0.52, 1.1, -13.0, sy + 0.26, sz, stairMat, { recv: true });
    S(-15.2, -10.8, sy, sy + 0.52, sz - 0.55, sz + 0.55, 'mezz_stair', true);
  }
  I(4.4, 0.3, 3.0, -13.0, MEZZ - 0.15, stairTop - 1.5, floorMat, { recv: true });
  S(-15.2, -10.8, MEZZ - 0.3, MEZZ, stairTop - 3, stairTop, 'mezz_stair', true);
  for (const tz of [-75, -57, -39, 55, 73]) {
    I(1.6, 0.08, 1.6, -16.5, MEZZ + 0.78, tz, woodMat);
    I(0.16, 0.74, 0.16, -16.5, MEZZ + 0.37, tz, ribMat);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
      I(0.9, 0.45, 0.9, -16.5 + s * 1.7, MEZZ + 0.23, tz, seatMat);
      I(0.9, 0.55, 0.16, -16.5 + s * 1.7, MEZZ + 0.72, tz + 0.4, seatMat);
    }
  }
  I(3.0, 1.1, 12.0, -19.4, MEZZ + 0.55, 0, woodMat, { cast: true });
  I(3.3, 0.12, 12.3, -19.4, MEZZ + 1.16, 0, topMat);
  S(-20.9, -17.9, MEZZ, MEZZ + 1.22, -6, 6, 'mezz_bar', true);
  neon(inn, 'SKY DECK  â€¢  BAR & GRILL', -15.0, MEZZ + 3.2, 0, '#1c1917', '#fcd34d', 10.0, 0.85);

  // Airside retail + food court
  I(9.0, 3.2, 13.0, 11.0, 1.6, -73, own(makeMat(0x7f1d1d, { texType: 'wood', repeatX: 3, repeatY: 2 })));
  I(9.4, 0.25, 13.4, 11.0, 3.3, -73, topMat);
  S(6.5, 15.5, 0, 3.2, -79.5, -66.5, 'duty_free', false);
  neon(inn, 'DUTY FREE', 11.0, 4.2, -79.8, '#0b1220', '#facc15', 7.0, 0.85);
  const shelfT = [];
  for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 6; c++) xform(shelfT, 6.9, 0.7 + r * 0.85, -78 + c * 2.0, 0.5, 0.55, 1.4);
  flushInstances(inn, UNIT_BOX, own(makeMat(0xf59e0b, { emissive: 0xb45309, emissiveIntensity: 0.3 })), shelfT);
  I(3.2, 1.1, 3.2, 11.0, 0.55, 75, woodMat, { cast: true });
  I(3.6, 0.2, 3.6, 11.0, 1.2, 75, topMat);
  S(9.4, 12.6, 0, 1.3, 73.4, 76.6, 'cafe_kiosk', true);
  neon(inn, 'SKY BEANS COFFEE', 11.0, 2.7, 73.2, '#1c1917', '#fcd34d', 5.0, 0.7);
  for (const tz of [-17, -5, 7, 19]) {
    I(1.5, 0.08, 1.5, 10.0, 0.76, tz, woodMat);
    I(0.16, 0.72, 0.16, 10.0, 0.36, tz, ribMat);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) I(0.85, 0.45, 0.85, 10.0, 0.23, tz + s * 1.6, seatMat);
  }
  const loungeT = [], backT = [];
  for (const gz of [-67, 45]) {
    for (let r = 0; r < 4; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 5; c++) {
      xform(loungeT, 15.5, 0.45, gz + r * 3.0 + c * 1.05, 3.4, 0.42, 0.9);
      xform(backT, 15.5, 0.85, gz + r * 3.0 + c * 1.05 + 0.45, 3.4, 0.7, 0.18);
    }
    S(13.8, 17.2, 0, 0.7, gz - 0.5, gz + 13.0, 'gate_seat', true);
  }
  flushInstances(inn, UNIT_BOX, seatMat, loungeT, { cast: true, recv: true });
  flushInstances(inn, UNIT_BOX, seatMat, backT);

  const planterG = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.75, 0.62, 0.9, 10);
  for (const [px, pz] of [[4.5, -45], [4.5, 45], [18.5, -5], [-5, 95]]) {
    const pl = new THREE.Mesh(planterG, own(makeMat(0x78350f, { roughness: 0.85 })));
    pl.position.set(px, 0.45, pz); inn.add(pl);
    const bush = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1.0, 0), own(makeMat(0x16a34a, { roughness: 0.9 })));
    bush.position.set(px, 1.5, pz); bush.castShadow = true; inn.add(bush);
    S(px - 0.75, px + 0.75, 0, 0.9, pz - 0.75, pz + 0.75, 'planter', true);
  }

  // Baggage carousel
  const carousel = new THREE.Group();
  carousel.position.set(-16.0, 0.55, 85);
  carousel.add(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(4.0, 4.0, 0.5, 20), beltMat));
  carousel.add(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.6, 1.6, 0.7, 16), ribMat));
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
    const a = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
    makeBox(carousel, 0.9, 0.6, 0.5, Math.cos(a) * 3.0, 0.5, Math.sin(a) * 3.0,
      own(makeMat([0xef4444, 0x2563eb, 0x16a34a, 0xf59e0b][i % 4], { roughness: 0.8 })), { ry: a });
  }
  inn.add(carousel);
  S(-20, -12, 0, 0.8, 81, 89, 'baggage_carousel', true);
  neon(inn, 'BAGGAGE CLAIM', -16.0, 3.6, 85, '#0b1220', '#a78bfa', 6.5, 0.8);

  const ceilT = [];
  for (let z = -HL + 9; z <= HL - 9; z += 9) for (const lx of [-14, 0, 14]) xform(ceilT, lx, TOP - 1.2, z, 7.0, 0.14, 0.8);
  flushInstances(inn, UNIT_BOX, glowMat, ceilT);
  for (const lz of doors) {
    const hl = new THREE.PointLight(0xfff1d6, 2.2, 70, 2);
    hl.position.set(0, 9.5, lz); inn.add(hl);
  }
  (aura.culls || []).push({ g: inn, x: TX, z: TZ, r: row.cullR ?? 135 });

  // =========================================================================
  // GATE PODS + PASSENGER BOARDING BRIDGES
  // =========================================================================
  for (const gt of gates) {
    const pod = new THREE.Group();
    pod.position.set(27.5, 0, gt.z);
    pod.userData.studioName = `Gate ${gt.n} hold-room`;
    g.add(pod);
    const P = (w, h, d, x, y, z, m, o) => makeBox(pod, w, h, d, x, y, z, m, o);
    const PS = (x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1, tag, climb) =>
      S(27.5 + x0, 27.5 + x1, y0, y1, gt.z + z0, gt.z + z1, tag, climb);

    P(11, 0.30, 28, 0, -0.15, 0, floorMat, { recv: true });
    PS(-5.5, 5.5, -0.30, 0.10, -14, 14, 'gate_pod_floor', true);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
      P(11, 8.0, 0.5, 0, 4.0, s * 14, white);
      PS(-5.5, 5.5, 0, 8.0, s * 14 - 0.25, s * 14 + 0.25, 'gate_pod_wall', false);
    }
    P(0.5, 8.0, 28, 5.5, 4.0, 0, curtain, { order: 3 });
    P(11.8, 0.5, 29, 0, 8.25, 0, shellMat, { cast: true });
    PS(-5.9, 5.9, 8.0, 8.5, -14.5, 14.5, 'gate_pod_roof', true);
    P(2.2, 1.15, 1.2, 2.6, 0.575, -6.0, deskMat, { cast: true });
    P(2.4, 0.1, 1.4, 2.6, 1.2, -6.0, topMat);
    PS(1.5, 3.7, 0, 1.25, -6.6, -5.4, 'gate_desk', true);
    neon(pod, `GATE ${gt.n}`, 2.6, 3.4, -6.0, '#0b1220', '#facc15', 3.6, 0.9);

    const seatT = [], bkT = [];
    for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 4; c++) {
      xform(seatT, -2.2, 0.45, 2.0 + r * 3.0 + c * 1.0, 3.0, 0.42, 0.85);
      xform(bkT, -2.2, 0.85, 2.45 + r * 3.0 + c * 1.0, 3.0, 0.7, 0.16);
    }
    flushInstances(pod, UNIT_BOX, seatMat, seatT, { cast: true, recv: true });
    flushInstances(pod, UNIT_BOX, seatMat, bkT);

    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      const sy = 0.10 + i * 0.36, sz = -8.0 + i * 1.0;
      P(4.6, 0.36, 1.0, 0.5, sy + 0.18, sz, stairMat, { recv: true });
      PS(-1.8, 2.8, sy, sy + 0.36, sz - 0.5, sz + 0.5, 'bridge_stair', true);
    }
    P(9.0, 0.30, 7.0, 1.0, DECK - 0.15, 1.5, floorMat, { recv: true });
    PS(-3.5, 5.5, DECK - 0.30, DECK, -2.0, 5.0, 'bridge_landing', true);
    P(0.16, 1.1, 7.0, -3.4, DECK + 0.55, 1.5, ribMat);

    const rot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(2.2, 2.2, 3.3, 14), shellMat);
    rot.position.set(33, DECK + 1.65, gt.z + 2); rot.castShadow = true; g.add(rot);
    const BL = 10.0, bz = gt.z + 2;
    B(g, BL, 0.24, 3.6, 38.0, DECK - 0.12, bz, topMat, { recv: true });
    S(33, 43, DECK - 0.24, DECK, bz - 1.8, bz + 1.8, 'bridge_deck', true);
    B(g, BL + 0.4, 0.22, 4.0, 38.0, DECK + 2.75, bz, shellMat, { cast: true });
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
      B(g, BL, 2.6, 0.14, 38.0, DECK + 1.35, bz + s * 1.85, curtain, { order: 3 });
      S(33, 43, DECK, DECK + 2.6, bz + s * 1.85 - 0.09, bz + s * 1.85 + 0.09, 'bridge_glass', false);
    }
    B(g, 0.5, 2.9, 3.8, 42.8, DECK + 1.35, bz, own(makeMat(0x1e293b, { roughness: 0.6 })));
    const leg = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.30, 0.36, DECK - 0.25, 8), ribMat);
    leg.position.set(40.5, (DECK - 0.25) / 2 + 0.25, bz); g.add(leg);
    const wheel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.42, 0.42, 0.3, 10), own(makeMat(0x1c1917)));
    wheel.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
    wheel.position.set(40.5, 0.42, bz + 1.2); g.add(wheel);

    enterableZones.push({
      minX: TX + 22.0, maxX: TX + 33.0, minZ: TZ + gt.z - 13.5, maxZ: TZ + gt.z + 13.5,
      name: `LAX GATE ${gt.n} HOLD-ROOM`
    });
    mapShapes.push({ x: TX + 27.5, z: TZ + gt.z, w: 11, d: 28, color: '#e2e8f0', border: '#facc15', enterable: true });
  }

  // Apron stand lead-in markings (equipment is separate rows)
  const leadT = [];
  for (const gt of gates) {
    for (let x = 44; x <= 74; x += 5) xform(leadT, x, -0.03, gt.z, 3.2, 0.03, 0.32);
    xform(leadT, 54, -0.03, gt.z - 16, 0.32, 0.03, 12);
    xform(leadT, 54, -0.03, gt.z + 16, 0.32, 0.03, 12);
  }
  flushInstances(g, UNIT_BOX, paint, leadT);

  enterableZones.push({ minX: TX - HW + 0.6, maxX: TX + 1.4, minZ: TZ - HL + 0.6, maxZ: TZ + HL - 0.6, name: 'LAX LANDSIDE DEPARTURES HALL' });
  enterableZones.push({ minX: TX - 2.0, maxX: TX + 6.0, minZ: TZ + secLo, maxZ: TZ + secHi, name: 'LAX SECURITY CHECKPOINT' });
  enterableZones.push({ minX: TX + 2.6, maxX: TX + HW - 0.6, minZ: TZ - HL + 0.6, maxZ: TZ + HL - 0.6, name: 'LAX AIRSIDE CONCOURSE' });
  mapShapes.push({ x: TX, z: TZ, w: 2 * HW, d: 2 * HL, color: '#f8fafc', border: '#0284c7', enterable: true });

  return {
    object: g, boxes, interior: inn, board, carousel,
    anchors: { landside: [TX - HW - 2, Y, TZ], security: [TX + 2, Y, TZ], carousel: [TX - 16, Y, TZ + 85] }
  };
}

export function resetAirport() {
  for (const m of ownedMats) {
    if (m.map && m.map.isCanvasTexture) m.map.dispose();
    if (m.emissiveMap && m.emissiveMap.isCanvasTexture && m.emissiveMap !== m.map) m.emissiveMap.dispose();
    m.dispose();
  }
  ownedMats.length = 0;
}

export const AIRPORT_BUILDERS = { terminal_lax: buildAirportTerminal };
export default AIRPORT_BUILDERS;

// =============================================================================
// worldObjects/buildings.js â€” 17 BUILDING PREFABS
//
// Signatures match what worldObjects/index.js adapters expect:
//   house_walkin / house_suburban  -> ({ x, z, w, d, h, wallColor, ... })
//   shop_hotdog / shop_bank / shop_dealer -> (x, z)
//   everything else                -> (row)   [passthrough adapter]
//
// REMOVED vs the original (now separate layout rows, would double-spawn):
//   â€¢ hideout's tank            -> rr/tank
//   â€¢ armory's dumpster/pallet/crate/walllamp -> la/armory_* rows
//   â€¢ armory's display cases + alarm + hooks  -> worldSystems/armory.js
// =============================================================================
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { aura, aviationBeacons, enterableZones, mapShapes, registerBuildingLod } from '../state.js';
import { makeMat, regHigh, worldRoot } from '../engine.js';
import {
  UNIT_BOX, addRamp, addSolidBox, flushInstances, makeBox, resolveMat, xform
} from '../worldKit.js';
import {
  flatRoof, foundY, gableRoof, hexStr, mapRect, neon, shell, wallMat, wallWithWindow, zone
} from './shell.js';

const M = (hex, opts) => makeMat(hex, opts);

// =============================================================================
// HOUSES
// =============================================================================
export function buildWalkInHouse(o) {
  const w = o.w ?? 9.8, d = o.d ?? 8.6, h = o.h ?? 4.0;
  const { x, z } = o;
  const y = foundY(o.baseY, x, z, w, d, 0.18);
  const wm = wallMat(o.wallColor ?? 0xe2e8f0);
  const rm = M(o.roofColor ?? 0xb91c1c, { texType: 'shingles', repeatX: 4, repeatY: 4, bumpScale: 0.02 });

  const S = shell({
    x, z, y, w, d, h, t: 0.36, doorW: 2.6, doorH: 2.85, wallMat: wm,
    name: o.label || 'Walk-in house',
    back: [2.2, 1.35, 2.0], side: [1.8, 1.35, 2.0], front: [1.45, 1.3, 2.0],
    floorMat: M(0xb45309, { texType: 'wood', repeatX: 4, repeatY: 4, roughness: 0.55 })
  });
  const { g, solid, minX, maxX, minZ, maxZ } = S;

  // Foundation plinth (Frostpine lodge sits on 2.4 m of rock).
  if (o.foundation) {
    const f = o.foundation, fw = f.w ?? w + 1.6, fd = f.d ?? d + 1.6, fh = f.h ?? 2.4;
    makeBox(worldRoot, fw, fh, fd, x, y - fh / 2, z,
      M(0x57534e, { texType: 'redrock', repeatX: 3, repeatY: 1 }),
      { cast: true, recv: true, name: `${o.label || 'House'} foundation` });
    solid(x - fw / 2, x + fw / 2, y - fh, y, z - fd / 2, z + fd / 2, 'lodge_foundation', true);
  }

  // Door trim.
  const trim = M(0x78350f, { texType: 'wood' });
  for (const s of [-1, 1]) makeBox(g, 0.14, 2.85, 0.44, s * 1.3, 1.425, d / 2 - 0.18, trim);

  // Furniture â€” every collider explicit, exactly as the original.
  const sofa = M(0x334155, { texType: 'fabric', repeatX: 2, repeatY: 4, roughness: 0.95 });
  makeBox(g, 1.1, 0.72, 2.5, -w / 2 + 1.0, 0.42, -0.2, sofa);
  solid(x - w / 2 + 0.45, x - w / 2 + 1.55, y, y + 0.72, z - 1.45, z + 1.05, 'sofa', true);
  makeBox(g, 1.1, 0.62, 1.6, -1.5, 0.38, -0.2, trim);
  solid(x - 2.05, x - 0.95, y, y + 0.62, z - 1.0, z + 0.6, 'table', true);

  makeBox(g, 2.8, 0.94, 1.1, w / 2 - 1.9, 0.54, -d / 2 + 1.0, M(0xe2e8f0, { texType: 'siding' }));
  makeBox(g, 2.9, 0.09, 1.18, w / 2 - 1.9, 1.05, -d / 2 + 1.0,
    M(0x334155, { texType: 'granite', repeatX: 3, repeatY: 1, roughness: 0.16, clearcoat: 0.9, flatShading: false }));
  solid(x + w / 2 - 3.3, x + w / 2 - 0.5, y, y + 1.02, z - d / 2 + 0.45, z - d / 2 + 1.55, 'counter', true);
  makeBox(g, 0.95, 2.05, 0.95, w / 2 - 0.9, 1.1, -d / 2 + 2.3,
    M(0xdbeafe, { texType: 'brushedsteel', repeatX: 1, repeatY: 2, metalness: 0.9, roughness: 0.22, flatShading: false }));
  solid(x + w / 2 - 1.4, x + w / 2 - 0.4, y, y + 2.07, z - d / 2 + 1.8, z - d / 2 + 2.8, 'fridge', true);

  makeBox(g, 2.0, 0.55, 1.4, -w / 2 + 1.45, 0.35, -d / 2 + 1.15, M(0x92400e, { texType: 'wood' }));
  makeBox(g, 1.88, 0.22, 1.28, -w / 2 + 1.45, 0.68, -d / 2 + 1.15,
    M(0xf8fafc, { texType: 'fabric', repeatX: 2, repeatY: 2, roughness: 0.95 }));
  solid(x - w / 2 + 0.45, x - w / 2 + 2.45, y, y + 0.77, z - d / 2 + 0.45, z - d / 2 + 1.85, 'bed', true);
  makeBox(g, 3.4, 0.03, 2.5, -1.5, 0.09, -0.2,
    M(0x0284c7, { texType: 'fabric', repeatX: 4, repeatY: 3, roughness: 0.96 }));

  gableRoof(g, w, d, h, 2.3, 0.55, rm);
  solid(minX, maxX, y + h - 0.2, y + h, minZ, maxZ, 'roof', true);

  // Distance-culled interior lamp.
  const lod = new THREE.Group();
  const stand = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.05, 0.14, 1.5, 8), M(0xfbbf24, { metalness: 0.85 }));
  stand.position.set(-w / 2 + 0.9, 0.85, d / 2 - 1.2);
  const shade = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.28, 0.32, 10),
    M(0xfef08a, { emissive: 0xfacc15, emissiveIntensity: 0.6 }));
  shade.position.set(-w / 2 + 0.9, 1.65, d / 2 - 1.2);
  lod.add(stand, shade);
  g.add(lod);
  registerBuildingLod(x, z, lod);

  zone(o.label, minX + 0.3, maxX - 0.3, minZ + 0.3, maxZ - 0.2);
  mapRect(x, z, w, d, hexStr(o.wallColor ?? 0xe2e8f0), '#f59e0b', true);
  return { object: g, boxes: S.boxes, anchors: { door: [x, y, z + d / 2], floor: [x, y, z] } };
}

export function buildSuburbanHouseBase(o) {
  const w = o.w ?? 8.4, d = o.d ?? 7.4, h = o.h ?? 3.6;
  const { x, z } = o, rotY = o.rotY || 0, y = 0.18, t = 0.28;
  const wm = wallMat(o.wallColor ?? 0xfed7aa, 3, 2);
  const rm = M(o.roofColor ?? 0x9a3412, { texType: 'shingles', repeatX: 4, repeatY: 4, bumpScale: 0.02 });

  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.position.set(x, y, z);
  g.rotation.y = rotY;
  g.userData.studioName = 'Suburban house';
  worldRoot.add(g);

  makeBox(g, w - 0.2, 0.08, d - 0.2, 0, 0.04, 0, M(0x92400e, { texType: 'wood' }));
  makeBox(g, w, h, t, 0, h / 2, -d / 2 + t / 2, wm, { cast: true });

  const front = new THREE.Group();
  front.position.set(0, 0, d / 2 - t / 2);
  for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
    const fw = new THREE.Group();
    fw.position.x = s * w * 0.28;
    wallWithWindow(fw, w * 0.38, h, t, 1.45, 1.25, 1.9, wm);
    front.add(fw);
  }
  makeBox(front, w * 0.24, h, t, 0, h / 2, 0, wm);
  makeBox(front, 1.2, 2.2, 0.14, 0, 1.1, 0.1, M(0x78350f, { texType: 'wood' }));
  g.add(front);

  for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
    const sw = new THREE.Group();
    sw.position.set(s * (w / 2 - t / 2), 0, 0);
    sw.rotation.y = -s * Math.PI / 2;
    wallWithWindow(sw, d - t * 2, h, t, 1.5, 1.25, 1.9, wm);
    g.add(sw);
  }
  makeBox(g, 1.4, 0.65, 1.0, -1.6, 0.4, 1.2, M(0x78350f, { texType: 'wood' }));
  makeBox(g, 2.2, 0.72, 0.9, 1.6, 0.42, 0.8, M(0x334155, { texType: 'fabric' }));

  gableRoof(g, w, d, h, 2.2, 0.55, rm);
  makeBox(g, 3.4, 0.24, 1.8, 0, 2.25, d / 2 + 0.85,
    M(o.roofColor ?? 0x9a3412, { texType: 'shingles', repeatX: 2, repeatY: 1 }), { cast: true });

  const lod = new THREE.Group();
  makeBox(lod, 0.85, 2.2, 0.85, w * 0.28, h + 1.4, -d * 0.15,
    M(0x9a3412, { texType: 'brick', repeatX: 1, repeatY: 2, bumpScale: 0.02 }), { cast: true });
  g.add(lod);
  registerBuildingLod(x, z, lod);

  const c = Math.abs(Math.cos(rotY)), s = Math.abs(Math.sin(rotY));
  const ww = w * c + d * s, wd = w * s + d * c;
  const boxes = [addSolidBox(x - ww / 2, x + ww / 2, y, y + h, z - wd / 2, z + wd / 2, 'house_wall', false)];
  const ax = x + Math.sin(rotY) * (d / 2 + 0.85), az = z + Math.cos(rotY) * (d / 2 + 0.85);
  const aw = 3.4 * c + 1.8 * s, ad = 3.4 * s + 1.8 * c;
  boxes.push(addSolidBox(ax - aw / 2, ax + aw / 2, 2.25, 2.48, az - ad / 2, az + ad / 2, 'porch_awning', true));
  mapRect(x, z, ww, wd, hexStr(o.wallColor ?? 0xfed7aa), '#1e293b', false);
  return { object: g, boxes };
}

export function embellishHouse(o) {
  const w = o.w ?? 8.4, d = o.d ?? 7.4;
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.position.set(o.x, 0.18, o.z);
  g.rotation.y = o.rotY || 0;
  worldRoot.add(g);
  const wood = M(0xf1f5f9, { roughness: 0.7 }), green = M(0x14532d, { roughness: 0.8 });
  const add = (sx, sy, sz, m, px, py, pz) => makeBox(g, sx, sy, sz, px, py, pz, m, { cast: true });
  add(w * 0.36, 0.14, 1.4, M(0xa8a29e, { texType: 'concrete' }), 0, 0.07, d / 2 + 0.7);
  for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
    const wx = s * w * 0.28;
    add(0.34, 1.25, 0.06, green, wx - 0.98, 1.9, d / 2 + 0.05);
    add(0.34, 1.25, 0.06, green, wx + 0.98, 1.9, d / 2 + 0.05);
    add(1.4, 0.22, 0.3, M(0x78350f, { texType: 'wood' }), wx, 1.18, d / 2 + 0.18);
    add(1.3, 0.2, 0.24, M(0x22c55e), wx, 1.38, d / 2 + 0.18);
    const bush = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.55, 0), M(0x15803d));
    bush.position.set(s * w * 0.44, 0.4, d / 2 + 0.55);
    bush.castShadow = true;
    g.add(bush);
  }
  add(0.08, 1.0, 0.08, wood, w * 0.6, 0.5, d / 2 + 3);
  add(0.4, 0.28, 0.25, M(0x1d4ed8), w * 0.6, 1.1, d / 2 + 3);
  add(w + 2, 0.07, 0.07, wood, 0, 0.7, d / 2 + 4.2);
  add(w + 2, 0.07, 0.07, wood, 0, 0.42, d / 2 + 4.2);
  for (let fx = -w / 2 - 1; fx <= w / 2 + 1.01; fx += 0.9) {
    if (Math.abs(fx) > 1.0) add(0.08, 0.95, 0.08, wood, fx, 0.48, d / 2 + 4.2);
  }
  return g;
}

export function buildSuburbanHouse(o) {
  const rec = buildSuburbanHouseBase(o);
  embellishHouse(o);
  return rec;
}

// =============================================================================
// SHOPS â€” five fittings on one shell
// =============================================================================
export function buildHotdogStore(x, z) {
  const w = 11.4, d = 9.6, h = 4.2, y = 0.18;
  const S = shell({
    x, z, y, w, d, h, t: 0.36, doorW: 2.8, doorH: 2.9,
    wallMat: wallMat(0xfef08a), name: 'AURA HOTDOGS',
    side: [2.2, 1.4, 2.0], front: 'auto',
    floorMat: M(0xf8fafc, { texType: 'cobblestone', repeatX: 6, repeatY: 6, roughness: 0.5 })
  });
  const { g, solid, minX, maxX, minZ, maxZ } = S;
  const red = M(0xdc2626, { roughness: 0.45 });
  flatRoof(S, red, { over: 0.6, thick: 0.36 });

  // Roof mascot.
  const mascot = new THREE.Group();
  mascot.position.set(0, h + 1.15, 0.5);
  const bun = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.45, 2.6, 6, 10), M(0xd97706, { roughness: 0.6 }));
  bun.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
  bun.position.set(0, 0, 0.24);
  const bun2 = bun.clone();
  bun2.position.z = -0.24;
  const wiener = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.36, 3.1, 6, 10), M(0xb91c1c, { roughness: 0.35 }));
  wiener.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
  wiener.position.y = 0.12;
  makeBox(mascot, 2.9, 0.08, 0.12, 0, 0.48, 0, M(0xfacc15));
  mascot.add(bun, bun2, wiener);
  g.add(mascot);
  neon(g, 'AURA HOTDOGS', 0, 3.55, d / 2 + 0.08, '#991b1b', '#fef08a', 5.2, 1.05);

  makeBox(g, 4.4, 1.0, 1.0, -2.4, 0.55, -0.5, red);
  makeBox(g, 4.5, 0.1, 1.1, -2.4, 1.08, -0.5, M(0xf8fafc, { metalness: 0.7 }));
  solid(x - 4.6, x - 0.2, y, 1.25, z - 1.05, z + 0.05, 'hd_counter', true);
  makeBox(g, 6.8, 0.96, 1.1, 0, 0.54, -d / 2 + 1.15,
    M(0x334155, { texType: 'brushedsteel', metalness: 0.85 }));
  solid(x - 3.4, x + 3.4, y, 1.25, minZ + 0.6, minZ + 1.75, 'hd_back_counter', true);

  const vat = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.48, 0.44, 0.65, 14),
    M(0xe2e8f0, { metalness: 0.92, roughness: 0.18 }));
  vat.position.set(2.4, 1.32, -d / 2 + 1.15);
  const liquid = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.42, 0.42, 0.06, 14),
    M(0xf97316, { emissive: 0xea580c, emissiveIntensity: 0.55 }));
  liquid.position.set(2.4, 1.63, -d / 2 + 1.15);
  neon(g, 'SAUSAGE REFILL VAT  [E]', 2.4, 2.35, -d / 2 + 0.45, '#7f1d1d', '#fef08a', 3.4, 0.62);
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(0.85, 1.15, 22),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfacc15, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
  ring.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  ring.position.set(2.4, 0.10, -2.2);
  g.add(vat, liquid, ring);

  zone('AURA HOTDOGS STORE', minX + 0.3, maxX - 0.3, minZ + 0.3, maxZ - 0.2);
  mapRect(x, z, w, d, '#ef4444', '#facc15', true);
  return { object: g, boxes: S.boxes, anchors: { refill: [x + 2.4, y, z - 2.2] }, refill: { x: x + 2.4, y, z: z - 2.2, label: 'AURA HOTDOGS SAUSAGE VAT' } };
}

export function buildCarDealership(x, z) {
  const w = 15.6, d = 12.6, h = 4.8, y = 0.18;
  const dark = M(0x1e293b, { texType: 'concrete', repeatX: 5, repeatY: 2, roughness: 0.45 });
  const trim = M(0xf59e0b, { metalness: 0.85, roughness: 0.2 });
  const S = shell({
    x, z, y, w, d, h, t: 0.38, doorW: 4.8, doorH: 3.5, wallMat: dark,
    name: 'AURA EXOTIC MOTORS', mullions: false,
    side: [d - 3.2, 2.75, 1.95], front: 'auto',
    floorMat: M(0xf8fafc, { texType: 'marble', repeatX: 6, repeatY: 5, roughness: 0.16, clearcoat: 0.9, flatShading: false })
  });
  const { g, solid, minX, maxX, minZ, maxZ } = S;
  flatRoof(S, dark, { over: 0.8, thick: 0.38 });
  makeBox(g, w + 0.9, 0.28, d + 0.9, 0, h + 0.04, 0, trim);

  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(2.5, 2.85, 32),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xf59e0b, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
  ring.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  ring.position.set(0, 0.10, -0.4);
  g.add(ring);
  makeBox(g, 2.4, 0.95, 0.95, -5.6, 0.52, -4.4, M(0x0f172a));
  makeBox(g, 2.5, 0.08, 1.05, -5.6, 1.02, -4.4, trim);
  solid(x - 6.85, x - 4.35, y, 1.18, z - 4.9, z - 3.9, 'dealer_desk', true);
  solid(minX + 0.38, maxX - 0.38, y, 0.26, minZ + 0.38, maxZ - 0.38, 'dealer_floor', true);
  neon(g, 'AURA EXOTIC MOTORS', 0, 4.15, d / 2 + 0.12, '#090d14', '#f59e0b', 8.2, 1.15);

  zone('AURA EXOTIC MOTORS', minX + 0.3, maxX - 0.3, minZ + 0.3, maxZ - 0.2);
  mapRect(x, z, w, d, '#1e293b', '#f59e0b', true);
  return { object: g, boxes: S.boxes, anchors: { turntable: [x, y + 0.1, z - 0.4] } };
}

export function buildReserveBank(x, z) {
  const w = 16.4, d = 13.6, h = 6.2, y = 0.18;
  const marble = M(0xf8fafc, { texType: 'marble', repeatX: 5, repeatY: 3, roughness: 0.22, clearcoat: 0.85, flatShading: false });
  const granite = M(0x1e293b, { texType: 'granite', repeatX: 4, repeatY: 2, roughness: 0.25, flatShading: false });
  const gold = M(0xfbbf24, { metalness: 0.94, roughness: 0.15, clearcoat: 0.9, flatShading: false });
  const steel = M(0x94a3b8, { texType: 'brushedsteel', metalness: 0.94, roughness: 0.18, flatShading: false });
  const S = shell({
    x, z, y, w, d, h, t: 0.45, doorW: 3.2, doorH: 3.4, wallMat: marble,
    name: 'SUNNYVALE RESERVE BANK',
    side: [3.4, 2.2, 2.6], front: [2.8, 2.2, 2.6], floorMat: marble, floorT: 0.10
  });
  const { g, solid, minX, maxX, minZ, maxZ } = S;
  flatRoof(S, marble, { over: 1.2, thick: 0.48 });
  makeBox(g, w + 1.5, 0.35, d + 1.9, 0, h + 0.62, 0, gold);
  for (const cx of [-6.6, -2.6, 2.6, 6.6]) {
    const col = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.42, 0.48, h, 14), marble);
    col.position.set(cx, h / 2, d / 2 + 0.65);
    col.castShadow = true;
    g.add(col);
  }
  neon(g, 'SUNNYVALE RESERVE BANK', 0, 5.1, d / 2 + 0.92, '#0f172a', '#fbbf24', 8.4, 1.15);

  makeBox(g, 7.2, 1.05, 1.1, -3.2, 0.58, -1.2, marble);
  makeBox(g, 7.4, 0.12, 1.25, -3.2, 1.15, -1.2, granite);
  makeBox(g, 6.8, 1.1, 0.08, -3.2, 1.75, -1.2, resolveMat('glassWarm'));
  solid(x - 6.8, x + 0.4, y, 1.4, z - 1.8, z - 0.6, 'bank_counter', true);

  makeBox(g, 5.6, 3.6, 0.4, 4.8, 1.85, -d / 2 + 0.6, steel);
  makeBox(g, 0.4, 3.6, 4.4, 2.2, 1.85, -d / 2 + 2.6, steel);
  solid(x + 2.0, x + 2.4, y, 3.8, minZ + 0.4, minZ + 4.8, 'vault_wall', false);
  const door = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.35, 1.35, 0.35, 20), steel);
  door.rotation.set(Math.PI / 2, 0, 0.7);
  door.position.set(1.35, 1.75, -d / 2 + 4.8);
  const wheel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.42, 0.05, 8, 16), gold);
  wheel.position.set(1.35, 1.75, -d / 2 + 5.0);
  g.add(door, wheel);
  for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) {
    makeBox(g, 0.42, 0.18, 0.22, 4.2 + c * 0.52, 0.24 + r * 0.19, -d / 2 + 1.5, gold);
  }

  zone('SUNNYVALE RESERVE BANK', minX + 0.3, maxX - 0.3, minZ + 0.3, maxZ - 0.2);
  mapRect(x, z, w, d, '#f8fafc', '#f59e0b', true);
  return { object: g, boxes: S.boxes };
}

/**
 * Shell + interior fit-out ONLY. Display cases, dumpster, alarm and the [E]/[F]
 * hooks live in worldSystems/armory.js, which consumes the returned record.
 */
export function buildFirearmsArmory(row) {
  const x = row.at[0], z = row.at[1];
  const w = row.w ?? 20, d = row.d ?? 22, h = row.h ?? 5.2;
  const y = foundY(row.baseY, x, z, w, d, 0.24);
  const dark = M(0x27272a, { texType: 'concrete', repeatX: 5, repeatY: 2, roughness: 0.72 });
  const trim = M(0xdc2626, { metalness: 0.55, roughness: 0.35 });
  const S = shell({
    x, z, y, w, d, h, t: 0.4, doorW: 3.2, doorH: 3.0, wallMat: dark,
    name: 'AURA ARMORY', mullions: false,
    side: [3.0, 1.6, 2.6], front: 'auto',
    floorMat: M(0x3f3f46, { texType: 'concrete', repeatX: 6, repeatY: 6, roughness: 0.55 }), floorT: 0.10
  });
  const { g, solid, minX, maxX, minZ, maxZ } = S;
  flatRoof(S, dark, { over: 0.8, thick: 0.4 });
  makeBox(g, w + 1.0, 0.34, d + 1.0, 0, h + 0.02, 0, trim);
  neon(g, 'AURA ARMORY', 0, 4.35, d / 2 + 0.16, '#0b0b0f', '#ef4444', 9.0, 1.25);
  neon(g, 'FIREARMS  â€¢  AMMO  â€¢  MELEE', 0, 3.35, d / 2 + 0.16, '#0b0b0f', '#facc15', 8.0, 0.6);

  // Interior lives in a distance-culled group (adopted by worldStream).
  const inn = new THREE.Group();
  inn.userData.studioName = 'Armory interior';
  g.add(inn);
  const slat = M(0x52525b, { texType: 'corrugated', repeatX: 8, repeatY: 2, metalness: 0.5, roughness: 0.6 });
  const steel = resolveMat('steel');
  makeBox(inn, w - 1.2, 3.0, 0.14, 0, 2.0, -d / 2 + 0.55, slat);
  makeBox(inn, 1.5, 1.05, 6.0, w / 2 - 1.5, 0.55, 0.5, M(0x1c1917, { texType: 'wood', repeatX: 3, repeatY: 1 }));
  makeBox(inn, 1.7, 0.1, 6.2, w / 2 - 1.5, 1.12, 0.5, steel);
  makeBox(inn, 0.7, 0.45, 0.55, w / 2 - 1.5, 1.4, 2.0, M(0x0f172a, { metalness: 0.6 }));
  makeBox(inn, 0.5, 0.3, 0.05, w / 2 - 1.5, 1.5, 2.3, resolveMat('screen'));
  solid(x + w / 2 - 2.35, x + w / 2 - 0.65, y, y + 1.17, z - 2.5, z + 3.5, 'armory_counter', true);

  makeBox(inn, 3.0, 2.2, 0.6, -w / 2 + 2.0, 1.1, -d / 2 + 1.0, slat);
  for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 5; c++) {
    makeBox(inn, 0.42, 0.3, 0.34, -w / 2 + 0.9 + c * 0.52, 0.55 + r * 0.62, -d / 2 + 1.35,
      M(r % 2 ? 0xd97706 : 0xb91c1c, { roughness: 0.6 }));
  }
  const ax = row.ammo?.at?.[0] ?? x - 6.8, az = row.ammo?.at?.[1] ?? z - 8.4;
  const ammoBox = makeBox(inn, 1.1, 0.8, 0.9, ax - x, 0.4, az - z, M(0xd97706, { texType: 'wood' }), { cast: true });
  solid(ax - 0.55, ax + 0.55, y, y + 0.8, az - 0.45, az + 0.45, 'armory_ammo', true);
  neon(inn, `SAUSAGE AMMO  [E] $${row.ammo?.price ?? 60}`, ax - x, 1.5, az - z + 0.6, '#7f1d1d', '#fef08a', 2.8, 0.45);

  for (const lx of [-5.5, 0, 5.5]) {
    makeBox(inn, 3.4, 0.1, 0.5, lx, h - 0.35, 0, resolveMat('stripLight'));
  }
  const shopLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xfff1d6, 2.4, 26, 2);
  shopLight.position.set(0, h - 1.0, 0);
  inn.add(shopLight);
  neon(inn, 'LICENSED DEALER  â€¢  NO REFUNDS', 0, 3.9, -d / 2 + 0.7, '#0b0b0f', '#a1a1aa', 7.0, 0.5);
  (aura.culls || []).push({ g: inn, x, z, r: 80 });

  solid(minX + 0.4, maxX - 0.4, y, y + 0.10, minZ + 0.4, maxZ - 0.4, 'armory_floor', true);
  zone('AURA ARMORY', minX + 0.4, maxX - 0.4, minZ + 0.4, maxZ - 0.3);
  mapRect(x, z, w, d, '#dc2626', '#facc15', true);
  return {
    object: g, boxes: S.boxes, interior: inn, ammoBox,
    anchors: { door: [x, y, z + d / 2], ammo: [ax, y, az], counter: [x + w / 2 - 1.5, y, z + 0.5] },
    armory: { x, z, w, d, h, baseY: y }
  };
}

// =============================================================================

// =============================================================================
export function buildDiner(row) {
  const [x, z] = row.at, w = row.w ?? 9.6, d = row.d ?? 9.2, h = row.h ?? 4.4, y = 0.18;
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.userData.studioName = row.name || 'Diner';
  worldRoot.add(g);
  makeBox(g, w, h, d, x, y + h / 2, z,
    M(row.wall ?? 0xfde68a, { texType: 'brick', repeatX: 4, repeatY: 2, bumpScale: 0.02 }), { cast: true });
  makeBox(g, w * 0.73, 1.8, 0.08, x, 1.5, z + d / 2 + 0.02, resolveMat('glassWarm'));
  neon(g, row.name || 'DINER', x, 3.65, z + d / 2 + 0.08,
    row.sign?.bg || '#7f1d1d', row.sign?.fg || '#fef08a');
  const box = addSolidBox(x - w / 2, x + w / 2, y, y + h, z - d / 2, z + d / 2, 'diner_roof', true);
  mapRect(x, z, w, d, hexStr(row.wall ?? 0xfde68a), '#b45309', false);
  return { object: g, boxes: [box] };
}

export function buildApartmentBlock(row) {
  const [x, z] = row.at, w = row.w ?? 11, d = row.d ?? 16, h = row.h ?? 8.4, y = 0.18;
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.userData.studioName = row.name || 'Apartment block';
  worldRoot.add(g);
  makeBox(g, w, h, d, x, y + h / 2, z,
    M(row.wall ?? 0xb45309, { texType: 'brick', repeatX: 5, repeatY: 4, bumpScale: 0.025 }),
    { cast: true, recv: true });
  const boxes = [addSolidBox(x - w / 2, x + w / 2, y, y + h, z - d / 2, z + d / 2, 'apartment_roof', true)];
  mapRect(x, z, w, d, hexStr(row.wall ?? 0xb45309), '#78350f', false);

  const fe = row.fireEscape;
  if (fe) {
    const steel = M(0x94a3b8, { texType: 'diamondplate', repeatX: 2, repeatY: 3, metalness: 0.88, roughness: 0.28, bumpScale: 0.02 });
    for (const st of fe.steps || []) {
      makeBox(g, 2.2, 0.25, 3.4, fe.x, st.y, st.z, steel, { cast: true });
      boxes.push(addSolidBox(fe.x - 1.1, fe.x + 1.1, st.y - 0.15, st.y + 0.13, st.z - 1.7, st.z + 1.7, 'fire_escape', true));
    }
  }
  return { object: g, boxes };
}

export function buildContainerStack(row) {
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.userData.studioName = row.name || 'Container stack';
  worldRoot.add(g);
  const boxes = [];
  const mat = (hex) => M(hex, { texType: 'corrugated', repeatX: 3, repeatY: 2, metalness: 0.82, roughness: 0.34, bumpScale: 0.03 });
  let cx = 0, cz = 0, n = 0;
  for (const [w, h, d, x, y, z, hex] of row.rows || []) {
    makeBox(g, w, h, d, x, y, z, mat(hex), { cast: true });
    boxes.push(addSolidBox(x - w / 2, x + w / 2, y - h / 2, y + h / 2, z - d / 2, z + d / 2, 'container', true));
    cx += x; cz += z; n++;
  }
  const cw = row.catwalk;
  if (cw) {
    makeBox(g, cw.w, 0.22, cw.d, cw.at[0], cw.y, cw.at[1],
      M(0x94a3b8, { texType: 'diamondplate', repeatX: 2, repeatY: 3, metalness: 0.88, roughness: 0.28 }), { cast: true });
    boxes.push(addSolidBox(cw.at[0] - cw.w / 2, cw.at[0] + cw.w / 2, cw.y - 0.12, cw.y + 0.11,
      cw.at[1] - cw.d / 2, cw.at[1] + cw.d / 2, 'gantry_catwalk', true));
  }
  if (n) mapRect(cx / n, cz / n, 7, 8, '#dc2626', '#991b1b', false);
  return { object: g, boxes };
}

// =============================================================================

// =============================================================================
export function buildMesa(row) {
  const [x, z] = row.at, w = row.w, d = row.d, h = row.h;
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.userData.studioName = `Mesa ${row.id}`;
  worldRoot.add(g);
  const body = M(0xb45309, { texType: 'redrock', repeatX: 6, repeatY: 3, roughness: 0.9, bumpScale: 0.03 });
  const cap = M(0xd97706, { texType: 'sand', repeatX: 4, repeatY: 4, roughness: 0.92 });
  makeBox(g, w, h, d, x, h / 2, z, body, { cast: true, recv: true });
  makeBox(g, w * 0.72, h * 0.55, d * 0.72, x, h + (h * 0.55) / 2, z, cap, { cast: true });
  const boxes = [
    addSolidBox(x - w / 2, x + w / 2, 0.02, h, z - d / 2, z + d / 2, 'mesa_low', true),
    addSolidBox(x - w * 0.36, x + w * 0.36, h, h * 1.55, z - d * 0.36, z + d * 0.36, 'mesa_top', true)
  ];
  mapRect(x, z, w, d, '#9a3412', '#7c2d12', false);
  return { object: g, boxes };
}

export function buildTemple(row) {
  const [x, z] = row.at;
  const pw = row.platW ?? 60, pd = row.platD ?? 60, ph = row.platH ?? 3;
  const y = row.baseY ?? 1.5;
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.userData.studioName = 'Ancient Sun Temple';
  worldRoot.add(g);
  const stone = M(0x57534e, { texType: 'cobblestone', repeatX: 8, repeatY: 8, roughness: 0.92 });
  const moss = M(0x3f6212, { texType: 'cobblestone', repeatX: 6, repeatY: 6, roughness: 0.95 });
  const boxes = [];
  makeBox(g, pw, ph, pd, x, y + ph / 2, z, stone, { cast: true, recv: true, name: 'Sun Temple platform' });
  boxes.push(addSolidBox(x - pw / 2, x + pw / 2, y, y + ph, z - pd / 2, z + pd / 2, 'temple_platform', true));

  const run = row.ramp?.run ?? 12, rw = row.ramp?.w ?? 12;
  makeBox(g, rw, 0.3, Math.hypot(run, ph), x, y + ph / 2 - 0.1, z + pd / 2 + run / 2,
    M(0x57534e, { texType: 'cobblestone', repeatX: 2, repeatY: 3 }), { recv: true, rx: Math.atan2(ph, run) });
  addRamp({ minX: x - rw / 2, maxX: x + rw / 2, minZ: z + pd / 2, maxZ: z + pd / 2 + run, startY: y + ph, endY: y, axis: 'z', dir: 1 });

  let ty = y + ph;
  (row.tiers || [[40, 40], [32, 32], [24, 24], [16, 16], [8, 8]]).forEach(([w, d], i) => {
    const th = 1.2;
    makeBox(g, w, th, d, x, ty + th / 2, z, i % 2 === 0 ? stone : moss, { cast: true, recv: true });
    boxes.push(addSolidBox(x - w / 2, x + w / 2, ty, ty + th, z - d / 2, z + d / 2, 'temple_tier', true));
    ty += th;
  });
  makeBox(g, 3, 1.6, 3, x, ty + 0.8, z, stone, { cast: true });
  boxes.push(addSolidBox(x - 1.5, x + 1.5, ty, ty + 1.6, z - 1.5, z + 1.5, 'temple_summit', true));
  if (row.relic) {
    const relic = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(1.2, 0), M(0xfacc15, { metalness: 0.9, roughness: 0.2 }));
    relic.position.set(x, ty + 2.8, z);
    relic.castShadow = true;
    g.add(relic);
  }
  neon(g, 'ANCIENT SUN TEMPLE', x, ty + 4.4, z + 4, '#14532d', '#fde047', 6.5, 1.0);
  mapRect(x, z, pw, pd, '#65a30d', '#facc15', false);
  return { object: g, boxes, anchors: { summit: [x, ty + 1.6, z] } };
}

export function buildHideout(row) {
  const [cx, cz] = row.at;
  const half = row.half ?? 30, peak = row.peak ?? 32, baseY = row.baseY ?? 0.02;
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.userData.studioName = 'REDROCK SCORPION HIDEOUT';
  worldRoot.add(g);
  const shellMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc28b47, roughness: 0.96, flatShading: true, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
  const chamber = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x34383b, roughness: 0.92 });
  const trim = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x9a6a32, roughness: 0.85 });
  const lightMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffc15a });
  const tri = (a, b, c) => {
    const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    geo.setFromPoints([new THREE.Vector3(...a), new THREE.Vector3(...b), new THREE.Vector3(...c)]);
    geo.setIndex([0, 1, 2]);
    geo.computeVertexNormals();
    const m = new THREE.Mesh(geo, shellMat);
    m.castShadow = true; m.receiveShadow = true;
    g.add(m);
  };
  const xw = cx - half, xe = cx + half, zn = cz - half, zs = cz + half, apex = [cx, peak, cz];
  tri([xw, baseY, zn], [xw, baseY, cz - 11], apex);
  tri([xw, baseY, cz + 11], [xw, baseY, zs], apex);
  tri([xw, 8.5, cz - 11], [xw, 8.5, cz + 11], apex);
  tri([xe, baseY, zn], [xe, baseY, zs], apex);
  tri([xw, baseY, zn], [xe, baseY, zn], apex);
  tri([xe, baseY, zs], [xw, baseY, zs], apex);

  const boxes = [];
  const box = (x, y, z, w, h, d, mat, solid = false) => {
    makeBox(g, w, h, d, x, y, z, mat, { cast: true, recv: true });
    if (solid) boxes.push(addSolidBox(x - w / 2, x + w / 2, baseY, baseY + h, z - d / 2, z + d / 2, 'solid', false));
  };
  box(cx, baseY + 0.06, cz, 54, 0.12, 54, chamber);
  box(cx, 8.1, cz, 50, 0.4, 50, chamber);
  box(cx, 4, cz - 25, 50, 8, 2, chamber, true);
  box(cx, 4, cz + 25, 50, 8, 2, chamber, true);
  box(cx + 25, 4, cz, 2, 8, 50, chamber, true);
  box(cx - 25, 4, cz - 20, 2, 8, 10, chamber, true);
  box(cx - 25, 4, cz + 20, 2, 8, 10, chamber, true);
  box(cx - 25, 7.8, cz, 2, 0.3, 30, chamber);
  box(cx - 42, baseY + 0.04, cz, 34, 0.08, 24, trim);
  box(cx - 24, 4, cz, 0.3, 0.3, 20, lightMat);
  box(cx, 4, cz - 24, 0.3, 0.3, 16, lightMat);

  const sg = new THREE.Group();
  sg.position.set(xw - 0.25, 7.5, cz);
  sg.rotation.y = -Math.PI / 2;
  neon(sg, 'REDROCK SCORPION HIDEOUT', 0, 0, 0, '#1c1917', '#fbbf24', 13, 1.25);
  g.add(sg);
  const beacon = new THREE.PointLight(0xffb14a, 55, 30, 2);
  beacon.position.set(cx, 6.5, cz);
  g.add(beacon);

  zone('REDROCK SCORPION HIDEOUT', cx - 24, cx + 24, cz - 24, cz + 24);
  mapRect(cx, cz, 60, 60, '#b77935', '#78350f', true);
  return { object: g, boxes, anchors: { pad: [cx + 1, 0.14, cz] } };
}

export function buildConcertHall(row) {
  const [x, z] = row.at, y = row.baseY ?? 0.30;
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.position.set(x, y, z);
  g.userData.studioName = row.sign || 'Concert hall';
  worldRoot.add(g);
  const steel = resolveMat('steel');
  makeBox(g, 24, 6.5, 22, 0, 3.25, 0, M(0xf8fafc, { texType: 'marble' }), { cast: true });
  for (const s of row.shells || []) {
    makeBox(g, s.w, s.h, s.d, s.at[0], s.at[1], s.at[2], steel,
      { cast: true, rx: s.rot[0], ry: s.rot[1], rz: s.rot[2] });
  }
  neon(g, row.sign || 'CONCERT HALL', 0, 5.2, 11.2, '#0f172a', '#38bdf8', 11.5, 1.15);
  const box = addSolidBox(x - 12, x + 12, y, y + 6.7, z - 11, z + 11, 'concert_hall', false);
  mapRect(x, z, 24, 22, '#e2e8f0', '#38bdf8', false);
  return { object: g, boxes: [box] };
}

export function buildArena(row) {
  const [x, z] = row.at, r = row.r ?? 14.5, h = row.h ?? 14.0, y = row.baseY ?? 0.30;
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.position.set(x, y, z);
  g.userData.studioName = row.sign || 'Arena';
  worldRoot.add(g);
  const bowl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r, r * 0.9, h, 24),
    M(0x1e293b, { metalness: 0.75, roughness: 0.25 }));
  bowl.position.y = h / 2; bowl.castShadow = true;
  const roof = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r * 0.79, r * 1.02, 3.2, 24),
    M(0x38bdf8, { metalness: 0.85, roughness: 0.18, emissive: 0x0284c7, emissiveIntensity: 0.22 }));
  roof.position.y = h + 1.6;
  const led = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r + 0.15, r + 0.15, 1.8, 24, 1, true),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: row.led ?? 0xa855f7, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
  led.position.y = h * 0.75;
  g.add(bowl, roof, led);
  neon(g, row.sign || 'ARENA', 0, 4.5, r + 0.3, '#1e1b4b', '#facc15', 13.5, 1.35);
  const box = addSolidBox(x - r, x + r, y, y + h + 3.2, z - r, z + r, 'aura_arena', false);
  mapRect(x, z, r * 2, r * 2, '#4f46e5', '#facc15', false);
  return { object: g, boxes: [box] };
}

export function buildHangar(row) {
  const [x, z] = row.at, w = row.w ?? 26, d = row.d ?? 28, h = row.h ?? 11, y = row.baseY ?? 0.29;
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.userData.studioName = row.name || 'Hangar';
  worldRoot.add(g);
  const steel = resolveMat('steel');
  makeBox(g, w, h, 0.6, x, y + h / 2, z - d / 2, steel, { cast: true });
  makeBox(g, w, h, 0.6, x, y + h / 2, z + d / 2, steel, { cast: true });
  makeBox(g, 0.6, h, d, x + w / 2, y + h / 2, z, steel, { cast: true });
  makeBox(g, w + 0.8, 0.6, d + 0.8, x, y + h + 0.3, z, M(0x334155), { cast: true });
  const boxes = [
    addSolidBox(x - w / 2, x + w / 2, y, y + h, z - d / 2, z - d / 2 + 0.6, 'hangar_wall', false),
    addSolidBox(x - w / 2, x + w / 2, y, y + h, z + d / 2 - 0.6, z + d / 2, 'hangar_wall', false),
    addSolidBox(x + w / 2 - 0.6, x + w / 2, y, y + h, z - d / 2, z + d / 2, 'hangar_wall', false),
    addSolidBox(x - w / 2, x + w / 2, y + h, y + h + 0.6, z - d / 2, z + d / 2, 'hangar_roof', true)
  ];
  neon(g, row.name || 'HANGAR', x, y + h - 1.6, z - d / 2 - 0.4, '#0f172a', '#facc15', 12.0, 1.1);
  zone('AIRCRAFT HANGAR', x - w / 2 + 1, x + w / 2 - 1, z - d / 2 + 1, z + d / 2 - 1);
  mapRect(x, z, w, d, '#94a3b8', '#1e293b', true);
  return { object: g, boxes };
}

export function buildThemeBuilding(row) {
  const [x, z] = row.at, y = row.baseY ?? 0.24;
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.position.set(x, y, z);
  g.userData.studioName = 'LAX Theme Building';
  worldRoot.add(g);
  const white = resolveMat('white');
  const core = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(2.6, 3.4, 15.0, 14), white);
  core.position.y = 7.5;
  const saucer = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(8.5, 6.8, 2.6, 20),
    M(0x38bdf8, { metalness: 0.85, roughness: 0.15, emissive: 0x0284c7, emissiveIntensity: 0.3 }));
  saucer.position.y = 10.2;
  const cap = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(8.8, 2.2, 20), white);
  cap.position.y = 12.6;
  g.add(core, saucer, cap);
  for (const ry of [0, Math.PI / 2]) {
    const pts = [];
    for (let t = -1; t <= 1.001; t += 0.1) pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(t * 16.5, (1 - t * t) * 18.5, 0));
    const arch = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TubeGeometry(new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(pts), 28, 0.55, 8, false), white);
    arch.rotation.y = ry;
    arch.castShadow = true;
    g.add(arch);
  }
  const box = addSolidBox(x - 3.5, x + 3.5, y, y + 15.0, z - 3.5, z + 3.5, 'lax_theme_bldg', false);
  mapRect(x, z, 34, 34, '#f8fafc', '#38bdf8', false);
  return { object: g, boxes: [box] };
}

export function buildATCTower(row) {
  const [x, z] = row.at, h = row.h ?? 34.0, y = row.baseY ?? 0.24;
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.userData.studioName = 'Control tower';
  worldRoot.add(g);
  const white = resolveMat('white');
  makeBox(g, 5.2, h, 5.2, x, y + h / 2, z, white, { cast: true });
  const cab = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(5.4, 3.8, 4.6, 12),
    M(0x10b981, { metalness: 0.85, roughness: 0.15, emissive: 0x059669, emissiveIntensity: 0.28 }));
  cab.position.set(x, y + h + 2.0, z);
  const roof = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(5.8, 5.8, 0.7, 12), white);
  roof.position.set(x, y + h + 4.6, z);
  const beacon = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.35, 8, 8), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xef4444 }));
  beacon.position.set(x, y + h + 7.0, z);
  aviationBeacons.push(beacon);
  g.add(cab, roof, beacon);
  neon(g, 'CONTROL TOWER', x, y + h - 4.0, z - 2.8, '#052e2b', '#6ee7b7', 5.0, 0.7);
  const box = addSolidBox(x - 2.6, x + 2.6, y, y + h + 5.0, z - 2.6, z + 2.6, 'atc_tower', false);
  mapRect(x, z, 11, 11, '#10b981', '#6ee7b7', false);
  return { object: g, boxes: [box] };
}
